Action: FileEditorAction

Arguments:
  command: "create"
  path: "/app/backend/store.js"
  file_text: 
    const crypto = require('crypto');
    const fs = require('fs');
    const path = require('path');
    const Database = require('better-sqlite3');
    
    const ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, '..');
    const SEED_PATH = '/recruitment/records/pellmoor_seed_data.json';
    const PIPELINE_STAGES = ['applied', 'screening', 'interview', 'offer', 
'hired'];
    const TERMINAL_STAGES = ['rejected', 'withdrawn'];
    const ELIGIBLE_PANEL_MEMBERS = new Set([
      'hiring@pellmoor.test',
      'panel1@pellmoor.test',
      'panel2@pellmoor.test',
    ]);
    const ROLE_BY_EMAIL = new Map([
      ['hiring@pellmoor.test', 'hiring manager'],
      ['panel1@pellmoor.test', 'panel'],
      ['panel2@pellmoor.test', 'panel'],
      ['coord@pellmoor.test', 'coordinator'],
    ]);
    
    function sha256(value) {
      return crypto.createHash('sha256').update(String(value)).digest('hex');
    }
    
    function nowIso() {
      return new Date().toISOString();
    }
    
    function parseJsonFile(filePath) {
      return JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(filePath, 'utf8'));
    }
    
    function normalizePayload(value) {
      if (Array.isArray(value)) {
        return value.map(normalizePayload);
      }
      if (value && typeof value === 'object') {
        return Object.keys(value)
          .sort()
          .reduce((acc, key) => {
            acc[key] = normalizePayload(value[key]);
            return acc;
          }, {});
      }
      return value;
    }
    
    function payloadHash(payload) {
      return sha256(JSON.stringify(normalizePayload(payload)));
    }
    
    function parsePositiveIntegerText(value) {
      if (typeof value !== 'string' || !/^\d+$/.test(value)) {
        return null;
      }
      return Number(value);
    }
    
    function allowedTargetStages(currentStage) {
      const index = PIPELINE_STAGES.indexOf(currentStage);
      if (index === -1) return [];
      const targets = new Set();
      if (PIPELINE_STAGES[index - 1]) targets.add(PIPELINE_STAGES[index - 1]);
      if (PIPELINE_STAGES[index + 1]) targets.add(PIPELINE_STAGES[index + 1]);
      targets.add('rejected');
      targets.add('withdrawn');
      return [...targets];
    }
    
    function isFrozenStage(stage) {
      return TERMINAL_STAGES.includes(stage) || stage === 'offer' || stage === 
'hired';
    }
    
    function createStore(dbPath) {
      const resolvedDbPath = dbPath || process.env.DB_PATH || path.join(ROOT, 
'pellmoor.db');
      const dbDir = path.dirname(resolvedDbPath);
      fs.mkdirSync(dbDir, { recursive: true });
      const db = new Database(resolvedDbPath);
      db.pragma('journal_mode = WAL');
      db.pragma('foreign_keys = ON');
      db.pragma('busy_timeout = 5000');
    
      initSchema(db);
      seedIfNeeded(db);
    
      function getSession(token) {
        if (!token) return null;
        const tokenHash = sha256(token);
        return db
          .prepare(
            `SELECT s.token_hash, s.email, s.created_at, s.revoked_at, u.name, 
u.role
             FROM sessions s
             JOIN users u ON u.email = s.email
             WHERE s.token_hash = ?`
          )
          .get(tokenHash);
      }
    
      function createSession(email) {
        const token = crypto.randomBytes(32).toString('hex');
        const tokenHash = sha256(token);
        const createdAt = nowIso();
        db.prepare(
          'INSERT INTO sessions(token_hash, email, created_at, revoked_at) 
VALUES(?, ?, ?, NULL)'
        ).run(tokenHash, email, createdAt);
        return { token, createdAt };
      }
    
      function revokeSession(token) {
        if (!token) return false;
        const tokenHash = sha256(token);
        const result = db.prepare('UPDATE sessions SET revoked_at = ? WHERE 
token_hash = ? AND revoked_at IS NULL').run(nowIso(), tokenHash);
        return result.changes > 0;
      }
    
      function getUserByEmail(email) {
        return db.prepare('SELECT email, name, role FROM users WHERE email = 
?').get(email);
      }
    
      function getVacancyRecord(code) {
        return db.prepare('SELECT code, title, team, openings, revision FROM 
vacancies WHERE code = ?').get(code);
      }
    
      function listVacancies() {
        const vacancyRows = db.prepare('SELECT code, title, team, openings, 
revision FROM vacancies ORDER BY code').all();
        return vacancyRows.map((vacancy) => summarizeVacancy(vacancy.code, 
vacancy));
      }
    
      function getBootstrap(email) {
        return {
          user: getUserByEmail(email),
          vacancies: listVacancies(),
          pipelineStages: PIPELINE_STAGES,
          terminalStages: TERMINAL_STAGES,
          panelMembers: [...ELIGIBLE_PANEL_MEMBERS].map((memberEmail) => 
getUserByEmail(memberEmail)).filter(Boolean),
        };
      }
    
      function getVacancyView(code) {
        const vacancy = getVacancyRecord(code);
        if (!vacancy) return null;
        return summarizeVacancy(code, vacancy, true);
      }
    
      function getCandidateView(candidateId) {
        const candidate = db
          .prepare(
            `SELECT c.id, c.vacancy_code, c.name, c.stage, c.assessment_version,
c.applied_at, c.created_at, c.updated_at,
                    v.title AS vacancy_title, v.team AS vacancy_team, 
v.openings, v.revision
             FROM candidates c
             JOIN vacancies v ON v.code = c.vacancy_code
             WHERE c.id = ?`
          )
          .get(candidateId);
        if (!candidate) return null;
        return buildCandidateDetail(candidate);
      }
    
      function previewBatch(vacancyCode, candidateIds) {
        const vacancy = getVacancyRecord(vacancyCode);
        if (!vacancy) return null;
        const candidateDetails = candidateIds.map((id) => getCandidateView(id));
        const currentCounts = vacancyCapacityCounts(vacancyCode);
        const projected = {
          reserved: currentCounts.reserved + candidateIds.length,
          filled: currentCounts.filled,
          available: vacancy.openings - (currentCounts.reserved + 
candidateIds.length + currentCounts.filled),
        };
        return {
          vacancy: summarizeVacancy(vacancyCode, vacancy, true),
          selectionCount: candidateIds.length,
          selection: candidateDetails,
          currentCapacity: currentCounts,
          projectedCapacity: projected,
        };
      }
    
      function createCandidate(actor, vacancyCode, name, noteText, operationId) 
{
        return runWriteOperation(actor, 'POST', 
`/api/vacancies/${vacancyCode}/candidates`, operationId, { vacancyCode, name, 
noteText }, () => {
          const vacancy = getVacancyRecord(vacancyCode);
          if (!vacancy) return businessReject(404, 'Vacancy not found');
          const cleanName = normalizeName(name);
          if (!cleanName) return businessReject(400, 'Candidate name is 
required');
          const duplicate = db
            .prepare('SELECT id FROM candidates WHERE vacancy_code = ? AND 
lower(name) = lower(?)')
            .get(vacancyCode, cleanName);
          if (duplicate) return businessReject(409, 'That person already exists 
on this vacancy');
          const candidateId = nextCandidateId();
          const createdAt = nowIso();
          const appliedAt = createdAt;
          const currentVersion = 1;
          const insertCandidate = db.prepare(
            `INSERT INTO candidates(id, vacancy_code, name, stage, 
assessment_version, applied_at, created_at, updated_at, created_by)
             VALUES(?, ?, ?, 'applied', ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`
          );
          insertCandidate.run(candidateId, vacancyCode, cleanName, 
currentVersion, appliedAt, createdAt, createdAt, actor.email);
          insertStageHistory({
            candidateId,
            vacancyCode,
            stage: 'applied',
            actorEmail: actor.email,
            reason: 'candidate created',
            assessmentVersion: currentVersion,
          });
          insertActivity({
            vacancyCode,
            candidateId,
            actorEmail: actor.email,
            type: 'candidate-created',
            title: `Added ${cleanName}`,
            body: `Created at applied stage`,
            revision: vacancy.revision + 1,
            details: {
              candidateId,
              stage: 'applied',
              assessmentVersion: currentVersion,
            },
          });
          if (noteText && String(noteText).trim()) {
            insertNote({
              actorEmail: actor.email,
              vacancyCode,
              candidateId,
              text: String(noteText).trim(),
            });
          }
          bumpVacancyRevision(vacancyCode);
          return success(201, { vacancy: getVacancyView(vacancyCode), candidate:
getCandidateView(candidateId) });
        });
      }
    
      function changeStage(actor, candidateId, nextStage, operationId) {
        return runWriteOperation(actor, 'POST', 
`/api/candidates/${candidateId}/stage`, operationId, { candidateId, nextStage },
() => {
          const candidate = getCandidateForUpdate(candidateId);
          if (!candidate) return businessReject(404, 'Candidate not found');
          const vacancy = getVacancyRecord(candidate.vacancy_code);
          if (!vacancy) return businessReject(404, 'Vacancy not found');
          if (actor.role !== 'hiring manager') return businessReject(403, 'Only 
Ruth may move candidates');
          if (candidate.stage === nextStage) return businessReject(409, 
'Candidate is already at that stage');
          if (TERMINAL_STAGES.includes(candidate.stage)) return 
businessReject(409, 'Terminal applications cannot be moved');
          if (![...PIPELINE_STAGES, ...TERMINAL_STAGES].includes(nextStage)) 
return businessReject(400, 'Unknown stage');
    
          const currentIndex = PIPELINE_STAGES.indexOf(candidate.stage);
          const nextIndex = PIPELINE_STAGES.indexOf(nextStage);
          const terminalMove = TERMINAL_STAGES.includes(nextStage);
          const legalStep = terminalMove || nextIndex === currentIndex - 1 || 
nextIndex === currentIndex + 1;
          if (!legalStep) return businessReject(409, 'Stage jumps are not 
allowed');
    
          const releasesOpening = candidate.stage === 'offer' || candidate.stage
=== 'hired';
          const takesOpening = nextStage === 'offer';
          const movesIntoInterview = nextStage === 'interview' && 
candidate.stage !== 'interview';
          const beforeVersion = candidate.assessment_version;
          const afterVersion = movesIntoInterview ? beforeVersion + 1 : 
beforeVersion;
          const currentRevision = vacancy.revision;
    
          if (candidate.stage === 'hired' && nextStage === 'interview') {
            return businessReject(409, 'Use hired to offer first, then offer to 
interview');
          }
    
          if (takesOpening) {
            const capacity = vacancyCapacityCounts(vacancy.code);
            if (capacity.available <= 0 && candidate.stage !== 'hired') {
              return businessReject(409, 'Vacancy is full');
            }
          }
    
          if (releasesOpening) {
            releaseOpening(candidate.stage, nextStage);
          }
    
          db.prepare('UPDATE candidates SET stage = ?, assessment_version = ?, 
updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?').run(nextStage, afterVersion, nowIso(), 
candidateId);
          insertStageHistory({
            candidateId,
            vacancyCode: vacancy.code,
            stage: nextStage,
            actorEmail: actor.email,
            reason: stageReason(candidate.stage, nextStage),
            assessmentVersion: afterVersion,
          });
          insertActivity({
            vacancyCode: vacancy.code,
            candidateId,
            actorEmail: actor.email,
            type: 'stage-change',
            title: `${candidate.name}: ${candidate.stage} → ${nextStage}`,
            body: movesIntoInterview ? `Assessment version advanced to 
${afterVersion}` : `Stage updated without changing assessment version`,
            revision: currentRevision + 1,
            details: {
              fromStage: candidate.stage,
              toStage: nextStage,
              assessmentVersionBefore: beforeVersion,
              assessmentVersionAfter: afterVersion,
              assessmentVersionChanged: movesIntoInterview,
            },
          });
          bumpVacancyRevision(vacancy.code);
          return success(200, { vacancy: getVacancyView(vacancy.code), 
candidate: getCandidateView(candidateId) });
        });
      }
    
      function changePanel(actor, candidateId, memberEmail, action, operationId)
{
        return runWriteOperation(actor, 'POST', 
`/api/candidates/${candidateId}/panel`, operationId, { candidateId, memberEmail,
action }, () => {
          const candidate = getCandidateForUpdate(candidateId);
          if (!candidate) return businessReject(404, 'Candidate not found');
          const vacancy = getVacancyRecord(candidate.vacancy_code);
          if (!vacancy) return businessReject(404, 'Vacancy not found');
          if (actor.role !== 'coordinator') return businessReject(403, 'Only Cal
may change panels');
          if (!['applied', 'screening', 'interview'].includes(candidate.stage)) 
{
            return businessReject(409, 'Panels are frozen at offer, hired, 
rejected and withdrawn');
          }
          if (!ELIGIBLE_PANEL_MEMBERS.has(memberEmail)) return 
businessReject(409, 'That person cannot be assigned to a panel');
          if (memberEmail === 'coord@pellmoor.test') return businessReject(409, 
'Cal cannot be assigned to a panel');
          const currentActive = getActivePanelMembers(candidateId);
          const alreadyActive = currentActive.includes(memberEmail);
          if (action === 'add' && alreadyActive) return businessReject(409, 
'That panel member is already assigned');
          if (action === 'remove' && !alreadyActive) return businessReject(409, 
'That panel member is not assigned');
          const beforeVersion = candidate.assessment_version;
          const afterVersion = beforeVersion + 1;
          if (action === 'add') {
            db.prepare(
              `INSERT INTO candidate_panels(candidate_id, vacancy_code, 
member_email, active, added_by, added_at, removed_by, removed_at)
               VALUES(?, ?, ?, 1, ?, ?, NULL, NULL)
               ON CONFLICT(candidate_id, member_email) DO UPDATE SET active = 1,
added_by = excluded.added_by, added_at = excluded.added_at, removed_by = NULL, 
removed_at = NULL`
            ).run(candidateId, vacancy.code, memberEmail, actor.email, 
nowIso());
          } else {
            db.prepare(
              `UPDATE candidate_panels SET active = 0, removed_by = ?, 
removed_at = ? WHERE candidate_id = ? AND member_email = ?`
            ).run(actor.email, nowIso(), candidateId, memberEmail);
          }
          db.prepare('UPDATE candidates SET assessment_version = ?, updated_at =
? WHERE id = ?').run(afterVersion, nowIso(), candidateId);
          insertStageHistory({
            candidateId,
            vacancyCode: vacancy.code,
            stage: candidate.stage,
            actorEmail: actor.email,
            reason: action === 'add' ? `panel member ${memberEmail} added` : 
`panel member ${memberEmail} removed`,
            assessmentVersion: afterVersion,
          });
          insertActivity({
            vacancyCode: vacancy.code,
            candidateId,
            actorEmail: actor.email,
            type: action === 'add' ? 'panel-add' : 'panel-remove',
            title: `${action === 'add' ? 'Added' : 'Removed'} panel member`,
            body: `${memberLabel(memberEmail)} ${action === 'add' ? 'assigned 
to' : 'removed from'} the panel`,
            revision: vacancy.revision + 1,
            details: {
              memberEmail,
              action,
              assessmentVersionBefore: beforeVersion,
              assessmentVersionAfter: afterVersion,
            },
          });
          bumpVacancyRevision(vacancy.code);
          return success(200, { vacancy: getVacancyView(vacancy.code), 
candidate: getCandidateView(candidateId) });
        });
      }
    
      function recordScore(actor, candidateId, score, operationId) {
        return runWriteOperation(actor, 'POST', 
`/api/candidates/${candidateId}/scores`, operationId, { candidateId, score }, ()
=> {
          const candidate = getCandidateForUpdate(candidateId);
          if (!candidate) return businessReject(404, 'Candidate not found');
          const vacancy = getVacancyRecord(candidate.vacancy_code);
          if (!vacancy) return businessReject(404, 'Vacancy not found');
          if (candidate.stage !== 'interview') return businessReject(409, 
'Scores can only be recorded at interview');
          if (!ELIGIBLE_PANEL_MEMBERS.has(actor.email)) return 
businessReject(403, 'Only panel members may score');
          const activePanel = getActivePanelMembers(candidateId);
          if (!activePanel.includes(actor.email)) return businessReject(403, 
'Only assigned panel members may score');
          if (!Number.isInteger(score) || score < 1 || score > 5) return 
businessReject(400, 'Scores must be whole numbers from 1 to 5');
          const version = candidate.assessment_version;
          const existing = db
            .prepare('SELECT score FROM candidate_scores WHERE candidate_id = ? 
AND assessment_version = ? AND member_email = ?')
            .get(candidateId, version, actor.email);
          db.prepare(
            `INSERT INTO candidate_scores(candidate_id, vacancy_code, 
assessment_version, member_email, score, recorded_by, recorded_at)
             VALUES(?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
             ON CONFLICT(candidate_id, assessment_version, member_email)
             DO UPDATE SET score = excluded.score, recorded_by = 
excluded.recorded_by, recorded_at = excluded.recorded_at`
          ).run(candidateId, vacancy.code, version, actor.email, score, 
actor.email, nowIso());
          insertActivity({
            vacancyCode: vacancy.code,
            candidateId,
            actorEmail: actor.email,
            type: existing ? 'score-updated' : 'score-recorded',
            title: `${memberLabel(actor.email)} scored ${score}`,
            body: `Assessment version ${version} now records a ${score} from 
${memberLabel(actor.email)}`,
            revision: vacancy.revision + 1,
            details: {
              memberEmail: actor.email,
              score,
              assessmentVersion: version,
              updated: Boolean(existing),
            },
          });
          bumpVacancyRevision(vacancy.code);
          return success(200, { vacancy: getVacancyView(vacancy.code), 
candidate: getCandidateView(candidateId) });
        });
      }
    
      function addNote(actor, candidateId, text, operationId) {
        return runWriteOperation(actor, 'POST', 
`/api/candidates/${candidateId}/notes`, operationId, { candidateId, text }, () 
=> {
          const candidate = getCandidateForUpdate(candidateId);
          if (!candidate) return businessReject(404, 'Candidate not found');
          const vacancy = getVacancyRecord(candidate.vacancy_code);
          if (!vacancy) return businessReject(404, 'Vacancy not found');
          const cleanText = normalizeNote(text);
          if (!cleanText) return businessReject(400, 'Note text is required');
          const noteId = insertNote({
            actorEmail: actor.email,
            vacancyCode: vacancy.code,
            candidateId,
            text: cleanText,
          });
          insertActivity({
            vacancyCode: vacancy.code,
            candidateId,
            actorEmail: actor.email,
            type: 'note',
            title: `Note added`,
            body: cleanText,
            revision: vacancy.revision + 1,
            details: { noteId },
          });
          bumpVacancyRevision(vacancy.code);
          return success(201, { vacancy: getVacancyView(vacancy.code), 
candidate: getCandidateView(candidateId) });
        });
      }
    
      function batchPreview(actor, vacancyCode, candidateIds) {
        if (actor.role !== 'hiring manager') {
          return businessReject(403, 'Only Ruth may review batch offers');
        }
        const vacancy = getVacancyRecord(vacancyCode);
        if (!vacancy) return businessReject(404, 'Vacancy not found');
        const selection = candidateIds.map((id) => getCandidateView(id));
        if (selection.some((item) => !item)) return businessReject(404, 'One or 
more candidates were not found');
        const currentCapacity = vacancyCapacityCounts(vacancyCode);
        const projectedCapacity = {
          reserved: currentCapacity.reserved + candidateIds.length,
          filled: currentCapacity.filled,
          available: vacancy.openings - currentCapacity.reserved - 
currentCapacity.filled - candidateIds.length,
        };
        return success(200, {
          vacancy: summarizeVacancy(vacancyCode, vacancy, true),
          selectionCount: candidateIds.length,
          selection: selection.map((candidate) => {
            const eligibility = evaluateOfferEligibility(candidate, vacancy);
            return {
              id: candidate.id,
              name: candidate.name,
              assessmentVersion: candidate.assessmentVersion,
              stage: candidate.stage,
              eligible: eligibility.ok,
              reasons: eligibility.reasons,
            };
          }),
          currentCapacity,
          projectedCapacity,
        }, false);
      }
    
      function confirmBatch(actor, vacancyCode, candidateIds, operationId) {
        return runWriteOperation(actor, 'POST', 
`/api/vacancies/${vacancyCode}/batch-offers`, operationId, { vacancyCode, 
candidateIds }, () => {
          if (actor.role !== 'hiring manager') return businessReject(403, 'Only 
Ruth may confirm batch offers');
          const vacancy = getVacancyRecord(vacancyCode);
          if (!vacancy) return businessReject(404, 'Vacancy not found');
          if (!Array.isArray(candidateIds) || candidateIds.length === 0) return 
businessReject(400, 'Select at least one candidate');
          if (candidateIds.some((id) => typeof id !== 'string' || !id.trim())) 
return businessReject(400, 'Candidate identifiers must be non-empty strings');
          if (new Set(candidateIds).size !== candidateIds.length) return 
businessReject(409, 'Duplicate candidate identifiers are not allowed');
          const currentCapacity = vacancyCapacityCounts(vacancyCode);
          if (candidateIds.length > currentCapacity.available) return 
businessReject(409, 'Vacancy does not have enough available openings');
    
          const selected = candidateIds.map((id) => getCandidateForUpdate(id));
          if (selected.some((candidate) => !candidate)) return 
businessReject(404, 'One or more candidates were not found');
          if (selected.some((candidate) => candidate.vacancy_code !== 
vacancyCode)) return businessReject(409, 'All candidates must belong to the 
selected vacancy');
          const stagingProblem = selected.find((candidate) => candidate.stage 
!== 'interview');
          if (stagingProblem) return businessReject(409, `${stagingProblem.name}
is not at interview`);
          for (const candidate of selected) {
            const eligibility = evaluateOfferEligibility(candidate, vacancy);
            if (!eligibility.ok) return businessReject(409, `${candidate.name} 
is not eligible for offer: ${eligibility.reasons.join('; ')}`);
          }
          const batchId = crypto.randomUUID();
          const currentRevision = vacancy.revision;
          const now = nowIso();
          selected.forEach((candidate, index) => {
            db.prepare('UPDATE candidates SET stage = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id
= ?').run('offer', now, candidate.id);
            insertStageHistory({
              candidateId: candidate.id,
              vacancyCode,
              stage: 'offer',
              actorEmail: actor.email,
              reason: 'batch offer confirmed',
              assessmentVersion: candidate.assessmentVersion,
              batchId,
              batchPosition: index + 1,
              batchSize: selected.length,
            });
            insertActivity({
              vacancyCode,
              candidateId: candidate.id,
              actorEmail: actor.email,
              type: 'batch-offer',
              title: `${candidate.name} offered in batch ${batchId}`,
              body: `Batch offer ${index + 1} of ${selected.length}`,
              revision: currentRevision + 1,
              batchId,
              batchPosition: index + 1,
              batchSize: selected.length,
              details: {
                candidateId: candidate.id,
                assessmentVersion: candidate.assessmentVersion,
                batchId,
                batchPosition: index + 1,
                batchSize: selected.length,
                toStage: 'offer',
              },
            });
          });
          bumpVacancyRevision(vacancyCode);
          return success(200, {
            vacancy: getVacancyView(vacancyCode),
            batchId,
            selection: selected.map((candidate, index) => ({
              id: candidate.id,
              name: candidate.name,
              position: index + 1,
              total: selected.length,
            })),
          });
        });
      }
    
      function runWriteOperation(actor, method, route, operationId, payload, 
handler) {
        const cleanOperationId = normalizeOperationId(operationId);
        if (!cleanOperationId) return businessReject(400, 'Operation id is 
required', false);
        const hash = payloadHash({ method, route, payload });
        const existing = db
          .prepare(
            `SELECT status_code, response_json, payload_hash
             FROM operations
             WHERE actor_email = ? AND operation_id = ? AND method = ? AND route
= ?`
          )
          .get(actor.email, cleanOperationId, method, route);
        if (existing) {
          if (existing.payload_hash !== hash) {
            return businessReject(409, 'That retry identity was already used for
different input');
          }
          return storedResult(existing.status_code, existing.response_json);
        }
        const tx = db.transaction(() => {
          const repeat = db
            .prepare(
              `SELECT status_code, response_json, payload_hash
               FROM operations
               WHERE actor_email = ? AND operation_id = ? AND method = ? AND 
route = ?`
            )
            .get(actor.email, cleanOperationId, method, route);
          if (repeat) {
            if (repeat.payload_hash !== hash) {
              return storedResult(409, JSON.stringify({ error: 'That retry 
identity was already used for different input' }));
            }
            return storedResult(repeat.status_code, repeat.response_json);
          }
          const result = handler();
          if (result.storeReceipt !== false) {
            db.prepare(
              `INSERT INTO operations(actor_email, operation_id, method, route, 
payload_hash, status_code, response_json, created_at)
               VALUES(?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`
            ).run(actor.email, cleanOperationId, method, route, hash, 
result.statusCode, JSON.stringify(result.body), nowIso());
          }
          return result;
        });
        return tx();
      }
    
      function success(statusCode, body, storeReceipt = true) {
        return { statusCode, body, storeReceipt };
      }
    
      function storedResult(statusCode, responseJson) {
        return { statusCode: Number(statusCode), body: JSON.parse(responseJson),
storeReceipt: false, replay: true };
      }
    
      function businessReject(statusCode, message, storeReceipt = true) {
        return success(statusCode, { error: message }, storeReceipt);
      }
    
      function normalizeName(name) {
        if (typeof name !== 'string') return '';
        return name.trim().replace(/\s+/g, ' ');
      }
    
      function normalizeNote(text) {
        if (typeof text !== 'string') return '';
        return text.trim();
      }
    
      function normalizeOperationId(operationId) {
        if (typeof operationId !== 'string') return '';
        const trimmed = operationId.trim();
        return trimmed.length ? trimmed : '';
      }
    
      function nextCandidateId() {
        const tx = db.transaction(() => {
          const current = db.prepare('SELECT value FROM counters WHERE name = 
?').get('candidate_seq');
          const next = current ? Number(current.value) + 1 : 110;
          db.prepare(
            `INSERT INTO counters(name, value) VALUES('candidate_seq', ?) ON 
CONFLICT(name) DO UPDATE SET value = excluded.value`
          ).run(next);
          return `CAND-${next}`;
        });
        return tx();
      }
    
      function getCandidateForUpdate(candidateId) {
        return db
          .prepare(
            `SELECT c.id, c.vacancy_code, c.name, c.stage, c.assessment_version,
c.applied_at, c.created_at, c.updated_at,
                    v.openings, v.revision
             FROM candidates c
             JOIN vacancies v ON v.code = c.vacancy_code
             WHERE c.id = ?`
          )
          .get(candidateId);
      }
    
      function insertStageHistory({ candidateId, vacancyCode, stage, actorEmail,
reason, assessmentVersion, batchId = null, batchPosition = null, batchSize = 
null }) {
        db.prepare(
          `INSERT INTO candidate_stage_history(candidate_id, vacancy_code, 
stage, actor_email, reason, assessment_version, batch_id, batch_position, 
batch_size, created_at)
           VALUES(?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`
        ).run(candidateId, vacancyCode, stage, actorEmail, reason, 
assessmentVersion, batchId, batchPosition, batchSize, nowIso());
      }
    
      function insertNote({ actorEmail, vacancyCode, candidateId, text }) {
        const result = db.prepare(
          `INSERT INTO candidate_notes(candidate_id, vacancy_code, actor_email, 
text, created_at)
           VALUES(?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`
        ).run(candidateId, vacancyCode, actorEmail, text, nowIso());
        return result.lastInsertRowid;
      }
    
      function insertActivity({ vacancyCode, candidateId = null, actorEmail, 
type, title, body, revision, batchId = null, batchPosition = null, batchSize = 
null, details = {} }) {
        db.prepare(
          `INSERT INTO activity_events(vacancy_code, candidate_id, actor_email, 
type, title, body, revision, batch_id, batch_position, batch_size, created_at, 
details_json)
           VALUES(?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`
        ).run(vacancyCode, candidateId, actorEmail, type, title, body, revision,
batchId, batchPosition, batchSize, nowIso(), JSON.stringify(details));
      }
    
      function bumpVacancyRevision(vacancyCode) {
        db.prepare('UPDATE vacancies SET revision = revision + 1 WHERE code = 
?').run(vacancyCode);
      }
    
      function releaseOpening(currentStage, nextStage) {
        if (currentStage === 'offer' || currentStage === 'hired') {
          if (nextStage !== 'offer' && nextStage !== 'hired') {
            return true;
          }
        }
        return false;
      }
    
      function stageReason(fromStage, toStage) {
        if (TERMINAL_STAGES.includes(toStage)) return `moved to ${toStage}`;
        if (toStage === 'interview' && fromStage !== 'interview') return 
'entered interview';
        if (fromStage === 'offer' && toStage === 'hired') return 'accepted 
offer';
        if (fromStage === 'hired' && toStage === 'offer') return 'returned from 
hired to offer';
        return `moved from ${fromStage} to ${toStage}`;
      }
    
      function getActivePanelMembers(candidateId) {
        return db
          .prepare('SELECT member_email FROM candidate_panels WHERE candidate_id
= ? AND active = 1 ORDER BY member_email')
          .all(candidateId)
          .map((row) => row.member_email);
      }
    
      function getPanelRows(candidateId) {
        return db
          .prepare(
            `SELECT member_email, active, added_by, added_at, removed_by, 
removed_at
             FROM candidate_panels
             WHERE candidate_id = ?
             ORDER BY member_email`
          )
          .all(candidateId);
      }
    
      function getCurrentScores(candidateId, version) {
        return db
          .prepare(
            `SELECT member_email, score, recorded_by, recorded_at
             FROM candidate_scores
             WHERE candidate_id = ? AND assessment_version = ?
             ORDER BY member_email`
          )
          .all(candidateId, version);
      }
    
      function getHistoricalScores(candidateId, currentVersion) {
        return db
          .prepare(
            `SELECT assessment_version, member_email, score, recorded_by, 
recorded_at
             FROM candidate_scores
             WHERE candidate_id = ? AND assessment_version < ?
             ORDER BY assessment_version DESC, member_email`
          )
          .all(candidateId, currentVersion);
      }
    
      function getNotes(candidateId) {
        return db
          .prepare(
            `SELECT id, actor_email, text, created_at
             FROM candidate_notes
             WHERE candidate_id = ?
             ORDER BY id ASC`
          )
          .all(candidateId);
      }
    
      function getActivity(vacancyCode, candidateId = null) {
        if (candidateId) {
          return db
            .prepare(
              `SELECT id, vacancy_code, candidate_id, actor_email, type, title, 
body, revision, batch_id, batch_position, batch_size, created_at, details_json
               FROM activity_events
               WHERE vacancy_code = ? AND candidate_id = ?
               ORDER BY id ASC`
            )
            .all(vacancyCode, candidateId)
            .map(formatActivity);
        }
        return db
          .prepare(
            `SELECT id, vacancy_code, candidate_id, actor_email, type, title, 
body, revision, batch_id, batch_position, batch_size, created_at, details_json
             FROM activity_events
             WHERE vacancy_code = ?
             ORDER BY id DESC
             LIMIT 50`
          )
          .all(vacancyCode)
          .map(formatActivity)
          .reverse();
      }
    
      function formatActivity(row) {
        return {
          id: row.id,
          vacancyCode: row.vacancy_code,
          candidateId: row.candidate_id,
          actorEmail: row.actor_email,
          type: row.type,
          title: row.title,
          body: row.body,
          revision: row.revision,
          batchId: row.batch_id,
          batchPosition: row.batch_position,
          batchSize: row.batch_size,
          createdAt: row.created_at,
          details: JSON.parse(row.details_json || '{}'),
        };
      }
    
      function summarizeVacancy(code, vacancyRow, includeCandidates = false) {
        const candidates = db
          .prepare(
            `SELECT id, name, stage, assessment_version, applied_at, created_at,
updated_at
             FROM candidates
             WHERE vacancy_code = ?
             ORDER BY
               CASE stage
                 WHEN 'applied' THEN 1
                 WHEN 'screening' THEN 2
                 WHEN 'interview' THEN 3
                 WHEN 'offer' THEN 4
                 WHEN 'hired' THEN 5
                 WHEN 'rejected' THEN 6
                 WHEN 'withdrawn' THEN 7
                 ELSE 99
               END,
               id`
          )
          .all(code);
        const capacity = vacancyCapacityCounts(code, candidates);
        const funnel = buildFunnel(code, candidates);
        const summaries = candidates.map((candidate) => 
buildCandidateSummary(candidate));
        const activity = getActivity(code);
        const vacancy = {
          code: vacancyRow.code,
          title: vacancyRow.title,
          team: vacancyRow.team,
          openings: vacancyRow.openings,
          revision: vacancyRow.revision,
          capacity,
          funnel,
          candidateCount: candidates.length,
          activity,
          summary: {
            pipelineCount: candidates.filter((candidate) => 
PIPELINE_STAGES.includes(candidate.stage)).length,
            terminalCount: candidates.filter((candidate) => 
TERMINAL_STAGES.includes(candidate.stage)).length,
          },
        };
        if (includeCandidates) {
          vacancy.candidates = summaries;
        }
        return vacancy;
      }
    
      function buildCandidateSummary(candidateRow) {
        const activePanel = getActivePanelMembers(candidateRow.id);
        const currentScores = getCurrentScores(candidateRow.id, 
candidateRow.assessment_version);
        const historicalScores = getHistoricalScores(candidateRow.id, 
candidateRow.assessment_version);
        const capacity = vacancyCapacityCounts(candidateRow.vacancy_code);
        const eligibility = evaluateOfferEligibility({
          id: candidateRow.id,
          name: candidateRow.name,
          stage: candidateRow.stage,
          assessmentVersion: candidateRow.assessment_version,
          activePanel,
          currentScores,
        }, getVacancyRecord(candidateRow.vacancy_code), capacity);
        return {
          id: candidateRow.id,
          name: candidateRow.name,
          stage: candidateRow.stage,
          assessmentVersion: candidateRow.assessment_version,
          appliedAt: candidateRow.applied_at,
          updatedAt: candidateRow.updated_at,
          activePanel,
          currentScoreCount: currentScores.length,
          historicalScoreCount: historicalScores.length,
          offerReady: eligibility.ok,
          blockedReasons: eligibility.reasons,
          currentScores,
          historicalScores: groupHistoricalScores(historicalScores),
          noteCount: getNotes(candidateRow.id).length,
        };
      }
    
      function buildCandidateDetail(candidateRow) {
        const activePanel = getActivePanelMembers(candidateRow.id);
        const candidatePanel = getPanelRows(candidateRow.id);
        const currentScores = getCurrentScores(candidateRow.id, 
candidateRow.assessment_version);
        const historicalScores = getHistoricalScores(candidateRow.id, 
candidateRow.assessment_version);
        const notes = getNotes(candidateRow.id);
        const activity = getActivity(candidateRow.vacancy_code, 
candidateRow.id);
        const vacancy = getVacancyRecord(candidateRow.vacancy_code);
        const capacity = vacancyCapacityCounts(candidateRow.vacancy_code);
        const eligibility = evaluateOfferEligibility({
          id: candidateRow.id,
          name: candidateRow.name,
          stage: candidateRow.stage,
          assessmentVersion: candidateRow.assessment_version,
          activePanel,
          currentScores,
        }, vacancy, capacity);
        return {
          id: candidateRow.id,
          name: candidateRow.name,
          stage: candidateRow.stage,
          assessmentVersion: candidateRow.assessment_version,
          vacancy: {
            code: candidateRow.vacancy_code,
            title: candidateRow.vacancy_title,
            team: candidateRow.vacancy_team,
            openings: candidateRow.openings,
            revision: candidateRow.revision,
          },
          appliedAt: candidateRow.applied_at,
          createdAt: candidateRow.created_at,
          updatedAt: candidateRow.updated_at,
          stageHistory: db
            .prepare(
              `SELECT stage, actor_email, reason, assessment_version, batch_id, 
batch_position, batch_size, created_at
               FROM candidate_stage_history
               WHERE candidate_id = ?
               ORDER BY id ASC`
            )
            .all(candidateRow.id)
            .map((row) => ({
              stage: row.stage,
              actorEmail: row.actor_email,
              reason: row.reason,
              assessmentVersion: row.assessment_version,
              batchId: row.batch_id,
              batchPosition: row.batch_position,
              batchSize: row.batch_size,
              createdAt: row.created_at,
            })),
          activePanel,
          panelAssignments: candidatePanel.map((row) => ({
            memberEmail: row.member_email,
            active: Boolean(row.active),
            addedBy: row.added_by,
            addedAt: row.added_at,
            removedBy: row.removed_by,
            removedAt: row.removed_at,
          })),
          currentScores,
          historicalScores: groupHistoricalScores(historicalScores),
          notes,
          activity,
          offer: {
            ready: eligibility.ok,
            blockedReasons: eligibility.reasons,
          },
          canScore: activePanel.includes(candidateRow.vacancy_code === 
'ROLE-014' ? 'hiring@pellmoor.test' : ''),
          capacity,
        };
      }
    
      function groupHistoricalScores(rows) {
        const grouped = new Map();
        for (const row of rows) {
          if (!grouped.has(row.assessment_version)) 
grouped.set(row.assessment_version, []);
          grouped.get(row.assessment_version).push({
            memberEmail: row.member_email,
            score: row.score,
            recordedBy: row.recorded_by,
            recordedAt: row.recorded_at,
          });
        }
        return [...grouped.entries()].map(([assessmentVersion, scores]) => ({
          assessmentVersion,
          scores,
        }));
      }
    
      function vacancyCapacityCounts(vacancyCode, candidateRows = null) {
        const rows = candidateRows || db
          .prepare('SELECT id, stage FROM candidates WHERE vacancy_code = ?')
          .all(vacancyCode);
        const reserved = rows.filter((row) => row.stage === 'offer').length;
        const filled = rows.filter((row) => row.stage === 'hired').length;
        const vacancy = getVacancyRecord(vacancyCode);
        const available = vacancy ? Math.max(0, vacancy.openings - reserved - 
filled) : 0;
        return { reserved, filled, available };
      }
    
      function buildFunnel(vacancyCode, candidateRows) {
        const histories = db
          .prepare(
            `SELECT candidate_id, stage, created_at
             FROM candidate_stage_history
             WHERE vacancy_code = ?
             ORDER BY id ASC`
          )
          .all(vacancyCode);
        const byCandidate = new Map();
        for (const row of histories) {
          if (!byCandidate.has(row.candidate_id)) 
byCandidate.set(row.candidate_id, []);
          byCandidate.get(row.candidate_id).push(row.stage);
        }
        const reached = new Map(PIPELINE_STAGES.map((stage) => [stage, new 
Set()]));
        const remain = new Map(PIPELINE_STAGES.map((stage) => [stage, 0]));
        const lost = new Map(PIPELINE_STAGES.map((stage) => [stage, 0]));
        for (const candidate of candidateRows) {
          const history = byCandidate.get(candidate.id) || [];
          const uniqueVisited = new Set(history.filter((stage) => 
PIPELINE_STAGES.includes(stage)));
          for (const stage of uniqueVisited) 
reached.get(stage).add(candidate.id);
          if (PIPELINE_STAGES.includes(candidate.stage)) {
            remain.set(candidate.stage, remain.get(candidate.stage) + 1);
          }
          if (TERMINAL_STAGES.includes(candidate.stage)) {
            const lastPipeline = [...history].reverse().find((stage) => 
PIPELINE_STAGES.includes(stage));
            if (lastPipeline) lost.set(lastPipeline, lost.get(lastPipeline) + 
1);
          }
        }
        return PIPELINE_STAGES.map((stage) => ({
          stage,
          reached: reached.get(stage).size,
          remain: remain.get(stage),
          lost: lost.get(stage),
        }));
      }
    
      function evaluateOfferEligibility(candidate, vacancy, capacity = null) {
        const reasons = [];
        if (!candidate || !vacancy) {
          return { ok: false, reasons: ['Missing candidate or vacancy'] };
        }
        if (candidate.stage !== 'interview') reasons.push('Candidate is not at 
interview');
        const activePanel = candidate.activePanel || 
getActivePanelMembers(candidate.id);
        const panelValid = isPanelValid(activePanel);
        if (!panelValid) reasons.push('Panel is not complete');
        const currentScores = candidate.currentScores || 
getCurrentScores(candidate.id, candidate.assessmentVersion);
        if (activePanel.some((memberEmail) => !currentScores.find((row) => 
row.member_email === memberEmail))) {
          reasons.push('Current panel scores are incomplete');
        }
        const cap = capacity || vacancyCapacityCounts(vacancy.code);
        if (cap.available <= 0) reasons.push('Vacancy is full');
        return { ok: reasons.length === 0, reasons };
      }
    
      function isPanelValid(activePanel) {
        const members = new Set(activePanel);
        if (members.size < 2) return false;
        if (members.size === 1 && members.has('hiring@pellmoor.test')) return 
false;
        if (members.size >= 2 && members.has('hiring@pellmoor.test') && 
members.size === 1) return false;
        if (members.size >= 2 && [...members].every((member) => member === 
'hiring@pellmoor.test')) return false;
        if (members.size >= 2 && members.size === 1) return false;
        if (members.size >= 2 && [...members].filter((member) => member !== 
'hiring@pellmoor.test').length === 0) return false;
        return true;
      }
    
      function memberLabel(email) {
        const user = getUserByEmail(email);
        return user ? `${user.name} (${email})` : email;
      }
    
      return {
        db,
        getSession,
        createSession,
        revokeSession,
        getUserByEmail,
        getBootstrap,
        getVacancyView,
        getCandidateView,
        listVacancies,
        createCandidate,
        changeStage,
        changePanel,
        recordScore,
        addNote,
        batchPreview,
        confirmBatch,
        parsePositiveIntegerText,
      };
    }
    
    function initSchema(db) {
      db.exec(`
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS metadata (
          key TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
          value TEXT NOT NULL
        );
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS users (
          email TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
          name TEXT NOT NULL,
          role TEXT NOT NULL,
          password_hash TEXT NOT NULL
        );
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS sessions (
          token_hash TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
          email TEXT NOT NULL,
          created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
          revoked_at TEXT,
          FOREIGN KEY(email) REFERENCES users(email)
        );
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS vacancies (
          code TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
          title TEXT NOT NULL,
          team TEXT NOT NULL,
          openings INTEGER NOT NULL,
          revision INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
          created_at TEXT NOT NULL
        );
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS candidates (
          id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
          vacancy_code TEXT NOT NULL,
          name TEXT NOT NULL,
          stage TEXT NOT NULL,
          assessment_version INTEGER NOT NULL,
          applied_at TEXT NOT NULL,
          created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
          updated_at TEXT NOT NULL,
          created_by TEXT NOT NULL,
          FOREIGN KEY(vacancy_code) REFERENCES vacancies(code)
        );
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS candidate_stage_history (
          id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
          candidate_id TEXT NOT NULL,
          vacancy_code TEXT NOT NULL,
          stage TEXT NOT NULL,
          actor_email TEXT NOT NULL,
          reason TEXT NOT NULL,
          assessment_version INTEGER NOT NULL,
          batch_id TEXT,
          batch_position INTEGER,
          batch_size INTEGER,
          created_at TEXT NOT NULL
        );
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS candidate_panels (
          candidate_id TEXT NOT NULL,
          vacancy_code TEXT NOT NULL,
          member_email TEXT NOT NULL,
          active INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
          added_by TEXT NOT NULL,
          added_at TEXT NOT NULL,
          removed_by TEXT,
          removed_at TEXT,
          PRIMARY KEY(candidate_id, member_email)
        );
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS candidate_scores (
          candidate_id TEXT NOT NULL,
          vacancy_code TEXT NOT NULL,
          assessment_version INTEGER NOT NULL,
          member_email TEXT NOT NULL,
          score INTEGER NOT NULL,
          recorded_by TEXT NOT NULL,
          recorded_at TEXT NOT NULL,
          PRIMARY KEY(candidate_id, assessment_version, member_email)
        );
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS candidate_notes (
          id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
          candidate_id TEXT NOT NULL,
          vacancy_code TEXT NOT NULL,
          actor_email TEXT NOT NULL,
          text TEXT NOT NULL,
          created_at TEXT NOT NULL
        );
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS activity_events (
          id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
          vacancy_code TEXT NOT NULL,
          candidate_id TEXT,
          actor_email TEXT NOT NULL,
          type TEXT NOT NULL,
          title TEXT NOT NULL,
          body TEXT NOT NULL,
          revision INTEGER NOT NULL,
          batch_id TEXT,
          batch_position INTEGER,
          batch_size INTEGER,
          created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
          details_json TEXT NOT NULL
        );
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS operations (
          actor_email TEXT NOT NULL,
          operation_id TEXT NOT NULL,
          method TEXT NOT NULL,
          route TEXT NOT NULL,
          payload_hash TEXT NOT NULL,
          status_code INTEGER NOT NULL,
          response_json TEXT NOT NULL,
          created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
          PRIMARY KEY(actor_email, operation_id, method, route)
        );
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS counters (
          name TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
          value INTEGER NOT NULL
        );
      `);
    }
    
    function seedIfNeeded(db) {
      const existing = db.prepare('SELECT value FROM metadata WHERE key = 
?').get('seed_complete');
      if (existing) return;
      const seed = parseJsonFile(SEED_PATH);
      const seededAt = seed.clock || nowIso();
      const passwordHash = sha256(seed.seed_password);
      const tx = db.transaction(() => {
        for (const person of seed.people) {
          db.prepare(
            'INSERT INTO users(email, name, role, password_hash) VALUES(?, ?, ?,
?)'
          ).run(person.email, person.name, person.role, passwordHash);
        }
        for (const role of seed.roles) {
          db.prepare(
            'INSERT INTO vacancies(code, title, team, openings, revision, 
created_at) VALUES(?, ?, ?, ?, 1, ?)'
          ).run(role.code, role.title, role.team, role.openings, seededAt);
        }
        const seq = db.prepare('INSERT INTO counters(name, value) VALUES(?, 
?)').run('candidate_seq', 109);
        for (const candidate of seed.candidates) {
          const currentVersion = 1;
          const createdAt = timeOffset(seededAt, candidate.days_since_applied, 
0);
          const appliedAt = createdAt;
          db.prepare(
            `INSERT INTO candidates(id, vacancy_code, name, stage, 
assessment_version, applied_at, created_at, updated_at, created_by)
             VALUES(?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`
          ).run(candidate.id, candidate.role, candidate.name, candidate.stage, 
currentVersion, appliedAt, createdAt, createdAt, 'seed');
          const history = candidate.history || [candidate.stage];
          history.forEach((stage, index) => {
            insertSeedStageHistory(db, candidate.id, candidate.role, stage, 
currentVersion, timeOffset(appliedAt, 0, index * 10));
            insertSeedActivity(db, candidate.role, candidate.id, 'seed', 
'seed-stage', `${candidate.name}: ${stage}`, `Imported stage ${stage}`, 1, {
              stage,
              candidateId: candidate.id,
              assessmentVersion: currentVersion,
              seed: true,
            }, timeOffset(appliedAt, 0, index * 10));
          });
        }
        for (const panel of seed.panels) {
          panel.members.forEach((memberEmail) => {
            db.prepare(
              `INSERT INTO candidate_panels(candidate_id, vacancy_code, 
member_email, active, added_by, added_at, removed_by, removed_at)
               VALUES(?, ?, ?, 1, ?, ?, NULL, NULL)`
            ).run(panel.candidate, candidateRole(db, panel.candidate), 
memberEmail, 'seed', seededAt);
            insertSeedActivity(db, candidateRole(db, panel.candidate), 
panel.candidate, 'seed', 'seed-panel', `${panel.candidate}: panel 
${memberEmail}`, `Imported panel member ${memberEmail}`, 1, {
              candidateId: panel.candidate,
              memberEmail,
              seed: true,
            }, seededAt);
          });
        }
        for (const score of seed.scores) {
          db.prepare(
            `INSERT INTO candidate_scores(candidate_id, vacancy_code, 
assessment_version, member_email, score, recorded_by, recorded_at)
             VALUES(?, ?, 1, ?, ?, ?, ?)`
          ).run(score.candidate, candidateRole(db, score.candidate), 
score.panel_member, score.score, 'seed', seededAt);
          insertSeedActivity(db, candidateRole(db, score.candidate), 
score.candidate, 'seed', 'seed-score', `${score.candidate}: score 
${score.score}`, `Imported score ${score.score} from ${score.panel_member}`, 1, 
{
            candidateId: score.candidate,
            memberEmail: score.panel_member,
            score: score.score,
            assessmentVersion: 1,
            seed: true,
          }, seededAt);
        }
        db.prepare('INSERT INTO metadata(key, value) VALUES(?, 
?)').run('seed_complete', '1');
      });
      tx();
    }
    
    function insertSeedStageHistory(db, candidateId, vacancyCode, stage, 
assessmentVersion, createdAt) {
      db.prepare(
        `INSERT INTO candidate_stage_history(candidate_id, vacancy_code, stage, 
actor_email, reason, assessment_version, batch_id, batch_position, batch_size, 
created_at)
         VALUES(?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, NULL, NULL, NULL, ?)`
      ).run(candidateId, vacancyCode, stage, 'seed', 'seed import', 
assessmentVersion, createdAt);
    }
    
    function insertSeedActivity(db, vacancyCode, candidateId, actorEmail, type, 
title, body, revision, details, createdAt) {
      db.prepare(
        `INSERT INTO activity_events(vacancy_code, candidate_id, actor_email, 
type, title, body, revision, batch_id, batch_position, batch_size, created_at, 
details_json)
         VALUES(?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, NULL, NULL, NULL, ?, ?)`
      ).run(vacancyCode, candidateId, actorEmail, type, title, body, revision, 
createdAt, JSON.stringify(details));
    }
    
    function timeOffset(baseIso, days, minutes) {
      const base = new Date(baseIso);
      base.setUTCDate(base.getUTCDate() - Number(days || 0));
      base.setUTCMinutes(base.getUTCMinutes() + Number(minutes || 0));
      return base.toISOString();
    }
    
    function candidateRole(db, candidateId) {
      const row = db.prepare('SELECT vacancy_code FROM candidates WHERE id = 
?').get(candidateId);
      return row ? row.vacancy_code : null;
    }

      createStore,
      PIPELINE_STAGES,
      TERMINAL_STAGES,
      ELIGIBLE_PANEL_MEMBERS,
      ROLE_BY_EMAIL,
    };

+34|function normalizePayload(value) {
+35|  if (Array.isArray(value)) {
+36|    return value.map(normalizePayload);
+37|  }
+38|  if (value && typeof value === 'object') {
+39|    return Object.keys(value)
+40|      .sort()
+41|      .reduce((acc, key) => {
+42|        acc[key] = normalizePayload(value[key]);
+43|        return acc;
+44|      }, {});
+45|  }
+46|  return value;
+47|}

+375|      db.prepare(
+376|        `INSERT INTO candidate_scores(candidate_id, vacancy_code, 
assessment_version, member_email, score, recorded_by, recorded_at)
+377|         VALUES(?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
+378|         ON CONFLICT(candidate_id, assessment_version, member_email)
+379|         DO UPDATE SET score = excluded.score, recorded_by = 
excluded.recorded_by, recorded_at = excluded.recorded_at`
+380|      ).run(candidateId, vacancy.code, version, actor.email, score, 
actor.email, nowIso());
+381|      insertActivity({
+382|        vacancyCode: vacancy.code,
+383|        candidateId,
+384|        actorEmail: actor.email,
+385|        type: existing ? 'score-updated' : 'score-recorded',
+386|        title: `${memberLabel(actor.email)} scored ${score}`,
+387|        body: `Assessment version ${version} now records a ${score} from 
${memberLabel(actor.email)}`,
+388|        revision: vacancy.revision + 1,
+389|        details: {
+390|          memberEmail: actor.email,
+391|          score,
+392|          assessmentVersion: version,
+393|          updated: Boolean(existing),
+394|        },
+395|      });
+396|      bumpVacancyRevision(vacancy.code);
+397|      return success(200, { vacancy: getVacancyView(vacancy.code), 
candidate: getCandidateView(candidateId) });
+398|    });
+399|  }
+400|
+401|  function addNote(actor, candidateId, text, operationId) {
+402|    return runWriteOperation(actor, 'POST', 
`/api/candidates/${candidateId}/notes`, operationId, { candidateId, text }, () 
=> {
+403|      const candidate = getCandidateForUpdate(candidateId);
+404|      if (!candidate) return businessReject(404, 'Candidate not found');
+405|      const vacancy = getVacancyRecord(candidate.vacancy_code);
+406|      if (!vacancy) return businessReject(404, 'Vacancy not found');
+407|      const cleanText = normalizeNote(text);
+408|      if (!cleanText) return businessReject(400, 'Note text is required');
+409|      const noteId = insertNote({
+410|        actorEmail: actor.email,
+411|        vacancyCode: vacancy.code,
+412|        candidateId,
+413|        text: cleanText,
+414|      });
+415|      insertActivity({
+416|        vacancyCode: vacancy.code,
+417|        candidateId,
+418|        actorEmail: actor.email,
+419|        type: 'note',
+420|        title: `Note added`,
+421|        body: cleanText,
+422|        revision: vacancy.revision + 1,
+423|        details: { noteId },
+424|      });
+425|      bumpVacancyRevision(vacancy.code);
+426|      return success(201, { vacancy: getVacancyView(vacancy.code), 
candidate: getCandidateView(candidateId) });
+427|    });
+428|  }
+429|
+430|  function batchPreview(actor, vacancyCode, candidateIds) {
+431|    if (actor.role !== 'hiring manager') {
+432|      return businessReject(403, 'Only Ruth may review batch offers');
+433|    }
+434|    const vacancy = getVacancyRecord(vacancyCode);
+435|    if (!vacancy) return businessReject(404, 'Vacancy not found');
+436|    const selection = candidateIds.map((id) => getCandidateView(id));
+437|    if (selection.some((item) => !item)) return businessReject(404, 'One or
more candidates were not found');
+438|    const currentCapacity = vacancyCapacityCounts(vacancyCode);
+439|    const projectedCapacity = {
+440|      reserved: currentCapacity.reserved + candidateIds.length,
+441|      filled: currentCapacity.filled,
+442|      available: vacancy.openings - currentCapacity.reserved - 
currentCapacity.filled - candidateIds.length,
+443|    };
+444|    return success(200, {
+445|      vacancy: summarizeVacancy(vacancyCode, vacancy, true),
+446|      selectionCount: candidateIds.length,
+447|      selection: selection.map((candidate) => {
+448|        const eligibility = evaluateOfferEligibility(candidate, vacancy);
+449|        return {
+450|          id: candidate.id,
+451|          name: candidate.name,
+452|          assessmentVersion: candidate.assessmentVersion,
+453|          stage: candidate.stage,
+454|          eligible: eligibility.ok,
+455|          reasons: eligibility.reasons,
+456|        };
+457|      }),
+458|      currentCapacity,
+459|      projectedCapacity,
+460|    }, false);
+461|  }
+462|
+463|  function confirmBatch(actor, vacancyCode, candidateIds, operationId) {
+464|    return runWriteOperation(actor, 'POST', 
`/api/vacancies/${vacancyCode}/batch-offers`, operationId, { vacancyCode, 
candidateIds }, () => {
+465|      if (actor.role !== 'hiring manager') return businessReject(403, 'Only
Ruth may confirm batch offers');
+466|      const vacancy = getVacancyRecord(vacancyCode);
+467|      if (!vacancy) return businessReject(404, 'Vacancy not found');
+468|      if (!Array.isArray(candidateIds) || candidateIds.length === 0) return
businessReject(400, 'Select at least one candidate');
+469|      if (candidateIds.some((id) => typeof id !== 'string' || !id.trim())) 
return businessReject(400, 'Candidate identifiers must be non-empty strings');
+470|      if (new Set(candidateIds).size !== candidateIds.length) return 
businessReject(409, 'Duplicate candidate identifiers are not allowed');
+471|      const currentCapacity = vacancyCapacityCounts(vacancyCode);
+472|      if (candidateIds.length > currentCapacity.available) return 
businessReject(409, 'Vacancy does not have enough available openings');
+473|
+474|      const selected = candidateIds.map((id) => getCandidateForUpdate(id));
+475|      if (selected.some((candidate) => !candidate)) return 
businessReject(404, 'One or more candidates were not found');
+476|      if (selected.some((candidate) => candidate.vacancy_code !== 
vacancyCode)) return businessReject(409, 'All candidates must belong to the 
selected vacancy');
+477|      const stagingProblem = selected.find((candidate) => candidate.stage 
!== 'interview');
+478|      if (stagingProblem) return businessReject(409, 
`${stagingProblem.name} is not at interview`);
+479|      for (const candidate of selected) {
+480|        const eligibility = evaluateOfferEligibility(candidate, vacancy);
+481|        if (!eligibility.ok) return businessReject(409, `${candidate.name} 
is not eligible for offer: ${eligibility.reasons.join('; ')}`);
+482|      }
+483|      const batchId = crypto.randomUUID();
+484|      const currentRevision = vacancy.revision;
+485|      const now = nowIso();
+486|      selected.forEach((candidate, index) => {
+487|        db.prepare('UPDATE candidates SET stage = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE 
id = ?').run('offer', now, candidate.id);
+488|        insertStageHistory({
+489|          candidateId: candidate.id,
+490|          vacancyCode,
+491|          stage: 'offer',
+492|          actorEmail: actor.email,
+493|          reason: 'batch offer confirmed',
+494|          assessmentVersion: candidate.assessmentVersion,
+495|          batchId,
+496|          batchPosition: index + 1,
+497|          batchSize: selected.length,
+498|        });
+499|        insertActivity({
+500|          vacancyCode,
+501|          candidateId: candidate.id,
+502|          actorEmail: actor.email,
+503|          type: 'batch-offer',
+504|          title: `${candidate.name} offered in batch ${batchId}`,
+505|          body: `Batch offer ${index + 1} of ${selected.length}`,
+506|          revision: currentRevision + 1,
+507|          batchId,
+508|          batchPosition: index + 1,
+509|          batchSize: selected.length,
+510|          details: {
+511|            candidateId: candidate.id,
+512|            assessmentVersion: candidate.assessmentVersion,
+513|            batchId,
+514|            batchPosition: index + 1,
+515|            batchSize: selected.length,
+516|            toStage: 'offer',
+517|          },
+518|        });
+519|      });
+520|      bumpVacancyRevision(vacancyCode);
+521|      return success(200, {
+522|        vacancy: getVacancyView(vacancyCode),
+523|        batchId,
+524|        selection: selected.map((candidate, index) => ({
+525|          id: candidate.id,
+526|          name: candidate.name,
+527|          position: index + 1,
+528|          total: selected.length,
+529|        })),
+530|      });
+531|    });
+532|  }
+533|
+534|  function runWriteOperation(actor, method, route, operationId, payload, 
handler) {
+535|    const cleanOperationId = normalizeOperationId(operationId);
+536|    if (!cleanOperationId) return businessReject(400, 'Operation id is 
required', false);
+537|    const hash = payloadHash({ method, route, payload });
+538|    const existing = db
+539|      .prepare(
+540|        `SELECT status_code, response_json, payload_hash
+541|         FROM operations
+542|         WHERE actor_email = ? AND operation_id = ? AND method = ? AND 
route = ?`
+543|      )
+544|      .get(actor.email, cleanOperationId, method, route);
+545|    if (existing) {
+546|      if (existing.payload_hash !== hash) {
+547|        return businessReject(409, 'That retry identity was already used 
for different input');
+548|      }
+549|      return storedResult(existing.status_code, existing.response_json);
+550|    }
+551|    const tx = db.transaction(() => {
+552|      const repeat = db
+553|        .prepare(
+554|          `SELECT status_code, response_json, payload_hash
+555|           FROM operations
+556|           WHERE actor_email = ? AND operation_id = ? AND method = ? AND 
route = ?`
+557|        )
+558|        .get(actor.email, cleanOperationId, method, route);
+559|      if (repeat) {
+560|        if (repeat.payload_hash !== hash) {
+561|          return storedResult(409, JSON.stringify({ error: 'That retry 
identity was already used for different input' }));
+562|        }
+563|        return storedResult(repeat.status_code, repeat.response_json);
+564|      }
+565|      const result = handler();
+566|      if (result.storeReceipt !== false) {
+567|        db.prepare(
+568|          `INSERT INTO operations(actor_email, operation_id, method, route,
payload_hash, status_code, response_json, created_at)
+569|           VALUES(?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`
+570|        ).run(actor.email, cleanOperationId, method, route, hash, 
result.statusCode, JSON.stringify(result.body), nowIso());
+571|      }
+572|      return result;
+573|    });
+574|    return tx();
+575|  }
+576|
+577|  function success(statusCode, body, storeReceipt = true) {
+578|    return { statusCode, body, storeReceipt };
+579|  }
+580|
+581|  function storedResult(statusCode, responseJson) {
+582|    return { statusCode: Number(statusCode), body: 
JSON.parse(responseJson), storeReceipt: false, replay: true };
+583|  }
+584|
+585|  function businessReject(statusCode, message, storeReceipt = true) {
+586|    return success(statusCode, { error: message }, storeReceipt);
+587|  }
+588|
+589|  function normalizeName(name) {
+590|    if (typeof name !== 'string') return '';
+591|    return name.trim().replace(/\s+/g, ' ');
+592|  }
+593|
+594|  function normalizeNote(text) {
+595|    if (typeof text !== 'string') return '';
+596|    return text.trim();
+597|  }
+598|
+599|  function normalizeOperationId(operationId) {
+600|    if (typeof operationId !== 'string') return '';
+601|    const trimmed = operationId.trim();
+602|    return trimmed.length ? trimmed : '';
+603|  }
+604|
+605|  function nextCandidateId() {
+606|    const tx = db.transaction(() => {
+607|      const current = db.prepare('SELECT value FROM counters WHERE name = 
?').get('candidate_seq');
+608|      const next = current ? Number(current.value) + 1 : 110;
+609|      db.prepare(
+610|        `INSERT INTO counters(name, value) VALUES('candidate_seq', ?) ON 
CONFLICT(name) DO UPDATE SET value = excluded.value`
+611|      ).run(next);
+612|      return `CAND-${next}`;
+613|    });
+614|    return tx();
+615|  }

+617|  function getCandidateForUpdate(candidateId) {
+618|    return db
+619|      .prepare(
+620|        `SELECT c.id, c.vacancy_code, c.name, c.stage, 
c.assessment_version, c.applied_at, c.created_at, c.updated_at,
+621|                v.openings, v.revision
+622|         FROM candidates c
+623|         JOIN vacancies v ON v.code = c.vacancy_code
+624|         WHERE c.id = ?`
+625|      )
+626|      .get(candidateId);
+627|  }

+631|      `INSERT INTO candidate_stage_history(candidate_id, vacancy_code, 
stage, actor_email, reason, assessment_version, batch_id, batch_position, 
batch_size, created_at)
+632|       VALUES(?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`
+633|    ).run(candidateId, vacancyCode, stage, actorEmail, reason, 
assessmentVersion, batchId, batchPosition, batchSize, nowIso());
+634|  }

+638|      `INSERT INTO candidate_notes(candidate_id, vacancy_code, actor_email,
text, created_at)
+639|       VALUES(?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`
+640|    ).run(candidateId, vacancyCode, actorEmail, text, nowIso());

type, title, body, revision, batchId = null, batchPosition = null, batchSize =

+646|      `INSERT INTO activity_events(vacancy_code, candidate_id, actor_email,
type, title, body, revision, batch_id, batch_position, batch_size, created_at, 
details_json)
+647|       VALUES(?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`
+648|    ).run(vacancyCode, candidateId, actorEmail, type, title, body, 
revision, batchId, batchPosition, batchSize, nowIso(), JSON.stringify(details));
+649|  }

+651|  function bumpVacancyRevision(vacancyCode) {
+652|    db.prepare('UPDATE vacancies SET revision = revision + 1 WHERE code = 
?').run(vacancyCode);
+653|  }
+654|
+655|  function releaseOpening(currentStage, nextStage) {
+656|    if (currentStage === 'offer' || currentStage === 'hired') {
+657|      if (nextStage !== 'offer' && nextStage !== 'hired') {
+658|        return true;
+659|      }
+660|    }
+661|    return false;
+662|  }
+663|
+664|  function stageReason(fromStage, toStage) {
+665|    if (TERMINAL_STAGES.includes(toStage)) return `moved to ${toStage}`;
+666|    if (toStage === 'interview' && fromStage !== 'interview') return 
'entered interview';
+667|    if (fromStage === 'offer' && toStage === 'hired') return 'accepted 
offer';
+668|    if (fromStage === 'hired' && toStage === 'offer') return 'returned from
hired to offer';
+669|    return `moved from ${fromStage} to ${toStage}`;
+670|  }

+672|  function getActivePanelMembers(candidateId) {
+673|    return db
+674|      .prepare('SELECT member_email FROM candidate_panels WHERE 
candidate_id = ? AND active = 1 ORDER BY member_email')
+675|      .all(candidateId)
+676|      .map((row) => row.member_email);
+677|  }
+678|
+679|  function getPanelRows(candidateId) {
+680|    return db
+681|      .prepare(
+682|        `SELECT member_email, active, added_by, added_at, removed_by, 
removed_at
+683|         FROM candidate_panels
+684|         WHERE candidate_id = ?
+685|         ORDER BY member_email`
+686|      )
+687|      .all(candidateId);
+688|  }

+690|  function getCurrentScores(candidateId, version) {
+691|    return db
+692|      .prepare(
+693|        `SELECT member_email, score, recorded_by, recorded_at
+694|         FROM candidate_scores
+695|         WHERE candidate_id = ? AND assessment_version = ?
+696|         ORDER BY member_email`
+697|      )
+698|      .all(candidateId, version);
+699|  }

+701|  function getHistoricalScores(candidateId, currentVersion) {
+702|    return db
+703|      .prepare(
+704|        `SELECT assessment_version, member_email, score, recorded_by, 
recorded_at
+705|         FROM candidate_scores
+706|         WHERE candidate_id = ? AND assessment_version < ?
+707|         ORDER BY assessment_version DESC, member_email`
+708|      )
+709|      .all(candidateId, currentVersion);
+710|  }

+712|  function getNotes(candidateId) {
+713|    return db
+714|      .prepare(
+715|        `SELECT id, actor_email, text, created_at
+716|         FROM candidate_notes
+717|         WHERE candidate_id = ?
+718|         ORDER BY id ASC`
+719|      )
+720|      .all(candidateId);
+721|  }
+722|
+723|  function getActivity(vacancyCode, candidateId = null) {
+724|    if (candidateId) {
+725|      return db
+726|        .prepare(
+727|          `SELECT id, vacancy_code, candidate_id, actor_email, type, title,
body, revision, batch_id, batch_position, batch_size, created_at, details_json
+728|           FROM activity_events
+729|           WHERE vacancy_code = ? AND candidate_id = ?
+730|           ORDER BY id ASC`
+731|        )
+732|        .all(vacancyCode, candidateId)
+733|        .map(formatActivity);
+734|    }
+735|    return db
+736|      .prepare(
+737|        `SELECT id, vacancy_code, candidate_id, actor_email, type, title, 
body, revision, batch_id, batch_position, batch_size, created_at, details_json
+738|         FROM activity_events
+739|         WHERE vacancy_code = ?
+740|         ORDER BY id DESC
+741|         LIMIT 50`
+742|      )
+743|      .all(vacancyCode)
+744|      .map(formatActivity)
+745|      .reverse();
+746|  }
