+748|  function formatActivity(row) {
+749|    return {
+750|      id: row.id,
+751|      vacancyCode: row.vacancy_code,
+752|      candidateId: row.candidate_id,
+753|      actorEmail: row.actor_email,
+754|      type: row.type,
+755|      title: row.title,
+756|      body: row.body,
+757|      revision: row.revision,
+758|      batchId: row.batch_id,
+759|      batchPosition: row.batch_position,
+760|      batchSize: row.batch_size,
+761|      createdAt: row.created_at,
+762|      details: JSON.parse(row.details_json || '{}'),
+763|    };
+764|  }
+765|
+766|  function summarizeVacancy(code, vacancyRow, includeCandidates = false) {
+767|    const candidates = db
+768|      .prepare(
+769|        `SELECT id, name, stage, assessment_version, applied_at, 
created_at, updated_at
+770|         FROM candidates
+771|         WHERE vacancy_code = ?
+772|         ORDER BY
+773|           CASE stage
+774|             WHEN 'applied' THEN 1
+775|             WHEN 'screening' THEN 2
+776|             WHEN 'interview' THEN 3
+777|             WHEN 'offer' THEN 4
+778|             WHEN 'hired' THEN 5
+779|             WHEN 'rejected' THEN 6
+780|             WHEN 'withdrawn' THEN 7
+781|             ELSE 99
+782|           END,
+783|           id`
+784|      )
+785|      .all(code);
+786|    const capacity = vacancyCapacityCounts(code, candidates);
+787|    const funnel = buildFunnel(code, candidates);
+788|    const summaries = candidates.map((candidate) => 
buildCandidateSummary(candidate));
+789|    const activity = getActivity(code);
+790|    const vacancy = {
+791|      code: vacancyRow.code,
+792|      title: vacancyRow.title,
+793|      team: vacancyRow.team,
+794|      openings: vacancyRow.openings,
+795|      revision: vacancyRow.revision,
+796|      capacity,
+797|      funnel,
+798|      candidateCount: candidates.length,
+799|      activity,
+800|      summary: {
+801|        pipelineCount: candidates.filter((candidate) => 
PIPELINE_STAGES.includes(candidate.stage)).length,
+802|        terminalCount: candidates.filter((candidate) => 
TERMINAL_STAGES.includes(candidate.stage)).length,
+803|      },
+804|    };
+805|    if (includeCandidates) {
+806|      vacancy.candidates = summaries;
+807|    }
+808|    return vacancy;
+809|  }
+810|
+811|  function buildCandidateSummary(candidateRow) {
+812|    const activePanel = getActivePanelMembers(candidateRow.id);
+813|    const currentScores = getCurrentScores(candidateRow.id, 
candidateRow.assessment_version);
+814|    const historicalScores = getHistoricalScores(candidateRow.id, 
candidateRow.assessment_version);
+815|    const capacity = vacancyCapacityCounts(candidateRow.vacancy_code);
+816|    const eligibility = evaluateOfferEligibility({
+817|      id: candidateRow.id,
+818|      name: candidateRow.name,
+819|      stage: candidateRow.stage,
+820|      assessmentVersion: candidateRow.assessment_version,
+821|      activePanel,
+822|      currentScores,
+823|    }, getVacancyRecord(candidateRow.vacancy_code), capacity);
+824|    return {
+825|      id: candidateRow.id,
+826|      name: candidateRow.name,
+827|      stage: candidateRow.stage,
+828|      assessmentVersion: candidateRow.assessment_version,
+829|      appliedAt: candidateRow.applied_at,
+830|      updatedAt: candidateRow.updated_at,
+831|      activePanel,
+832|      currentScoreCount: currentScores.length,
+833|      historicalScoreCount: historicalScores.length,
+834|      offerReady: eligibility.ok,
+835|      blockedReasons: eligibility.reasons,
+836|      currentScores,
+837|      historicalScores: groupHistoricalScores(historicalScores),
+838|      noteCount: getNotes(candidateRow.id).length,
+839|    };
+840|  }
+841|
+842|  function buildCandidateDetail(candidateRow) {
+843|    const activePanel = getActivePanelMembers(candidateRow.id);
+844|    const candidatePanel = getPanelRows(candidateRow.id);
+845|    const currentScores = getCurrentScores(candidateRow.id, 
candidateRow.assessment_version);
+846|    const historicalScores = getHistoricalScores(candidateRow.id, 
candidateRow.assessment_version);
+847|    const notes = getNotes(candidateRow.id);
+848|    const activity = getActivity(candidateRow.vacancy_code, 
candidateRow.id);
+849|    const vacancy = getVacancyRecord(candidateRow.vacancy_code);
+850|    const capacity = vacancyCapacityCounts(candidateRow.vacancy_code);
+851|    const eligibility = evaluateOfferEligibility({
+852|      id: candidateRow.id,
+853|      name: candidateRow.name,
+854|      stage: candidateRow.stage,
+855|      assessmentVersion: candidateRow.assessment_version,
+856|      activePanel,
+857|      currentScores,
+858|    }, vacancy, capacity);
+859|    return {
+860|      id: candidateRow.id,
+861|      name: candidateRow.name,
+862|      stage: candidateRow.stage,
+863|      assessmentVersion: candidateRow.assessment_version,
+864|      vacancy: {
+865|        code: candidateRow.vacancy_code,
+866|        title: candidateRow.vacancy_title,
+867|        team: candidateRow.vacancy_team,
+868|        openings: candidateRow.openings,
+869|        revision: candidateRow.revision,
+870|      },
+871|      appliedAt: candidateRow.applied_at,
+872|      createdAt: candidateRow.created_at,
+873|      updatedAt: candidateRow.updated_at,
+874|      stageHistory: db
+875|        .prepare(
+876|          `SELECT stage, actor_email, reason, assessment_version, batch_id,
batch_position, batch_size, created_at
+877|           FROM candidate_stage_history
+878|           WHERE candidate_id = ?
+879|           ORDER BY id ASC`
+880|        )
+881|        .all(candidateRow.id)
+882|        .map((row) => ({
+883|          stage: row.stage,
+884|          actorEmail: row.actor_email,
+885|          reason: row.reason,
+886|          assessmentVersion: row.assessment_version,
+887|          batchId: row.batch_id,
+888|          batchPosition: row.batch_position,
+889|          batchSize: row.batch_size,
+890|          createdAt: row.created_at,
+891|        })),
+892|      activePanel,
+893|      panelAssignments: candidatePanel.map((row) => ({
+894|        memberEmail: row.member_email,
+895|        active: Boolean(row.active),
+896|        addedBy: row.added_by,
+897|        addedAt: row.added_at,
+898|        removedBy: row.removed_by,
+899|        removedAt: row.removed_at,
+900|      })),
+901|      currentScores,
+902|      historicalScores: groupHistoricalScores(historicalScores),
+903|      notes,
+904|      activity,
+905|      offer: {
+906|        ready: eligibility.ok,
+907|        blockedReasons: eligibility.reasons,
+908|      },
+909|      canScore: activePanel.includes(candidateRow.vacancy_code === 
'ROLE-014' ? 'hiring@pellmoor.test' : ''),
+910|      capacity,
+911|    };
+912|  }
+913|
+914|  function groupHistoricalScores(rows) {
+915|    const grouped = new Map();
+916|    for (const row of rows) {
+917|      if (!grouped.has(row.assessment_version)) 
grouped.set(row.assessment_version, []);
+918|      grouped.get(row.assessment_version).push({
+919|        memberEmail: row.member_email,
+920|        score: row.score,
+921|        recordedBy: row.recorded_by,
+922|        recordedAt: row.recorded_at,
+923|      });
+924|    }
+925|    return [...grouped.entries()].map(([assessmentVersion, scores]) => ({
+926|      assessmentVersion,
+927|      scores,
+928|    }));
+929|  }
+930|
+931|  function vacancyCapacityCounts(vacancyCode, candidateRows = null) {
+932|    const rows = candidateRows || db
+933|      .prepare('SELECT id, stage FROM candidates WHERE vacancy_code = ?')
+934|      .all(vacancyCode);
+935|    const reserved = rows.filter((row) => row.stage === 'offer').length;
+936|    const filled = rows.filter((row) => row.stage === 'hired').length;
+937|    const vacancy = getVacancyRecord(vacancyCode);
+938|    const available = vacancy ? Math.max(0, vacancy.openings - reserved - 
filled) : 0;
+939|    return { reserved, filled, available };
+940|  }
+941|
+942|  function buildFunnel(vacancyCode, candidateRows) {
+943|    const histories = db
+944|      .prepare(
+945|        `SELECT candidate_id, stage, created_at
+946|         FROM candidate_stage_history
+947|         WHERE vacancy_code = ?
+948|         ORDER BY id ASC`
+949|      )
+950|      .all(vacancyCode);
+951|    const byCandidate = new Map();
+952|    for (const row of histories) {
+953|      if (!byCandidate.has(row.candidate_id)) 
byCandidate.set(row.candidate_id, []);
+954|      byCandidate.get(row.candidate_id).push(row.stage);
+955|    }
+956|    const reached = new Map(PIPELINE_STAGES.map((stage) => [stage, new 
Set()]));
+957|    const remain = new Map(PIPELINE_STAGES.map((stage) => [stage, 0]));
+958|    const lost = new Map(PIPELINE_STAGES.map((stage) => [stage, 0]));
+959|    for (const candidate of candidateRows) {
+960|      const history = byCandidate.get(candidate.id) || [];
+961|      const uniqueVisited = new Set(history.filter((stage) => 
PIPELINE_STAGES.includes(stage)));
+962|      for (const stage of uniqueVisited) 
reached.get(stage).add(candidate.id);
+963|      if (PIPELINE_STAGES.includes(candidate.stage)) {
+964|        remain.set(candidate.stage, remain.get(candidate.stage) + 1);
+965|      }
+966|      if (TERMINAL_STAGES.includes(candidate.stage)) {
+967|        const lastPipeline = [...history].reverse().find((stage) => 
PIPELINE_STAGES.includes(stage));
+968|        if (lastPipeline) lost.set(lastPipeline, lost.get(lastPipeline) + 
1);
+969|      }
+970|    }
+971|    return PIPELINE_STAGES.map((stage) => ({
+972|      stage,
+973|      reached: reached.get(stage).size,
+974|      remain: remain.get(stage),
+975|      lost: lost.get(stage),
+976|    }));
+977|  }
+978|
+979|  function evaluateOfferEligibility(candidate, vacancy, capacity = null) {
+980|    const reasons = [];
+981|    if (!candidate || !vacancy) {
+982|      return { ok: false, reasons: ['Missing candidate or vacancy'] };
+983|    }
+984|    if (candidate.stage !== 'interview') reasons.push('Candidate is not at 
interview');
+985|    const activePanel = candidate.activePanel || 
getActivePanelMembers(candidate.id);
+986|    const panelValid = isPanelValid(activePanel);
+987|    if (!panelValid) reasons.push('Panel is not complete');
+988|    const currentScores = candidate.currentScores || 
getCurrentScores(candidate.id, candidate.assessmentVersion);
+989|    if (activePanel.some((memberEmail) => !currentScores.find((row) => 
row.member_email === memberEmail))) {
+990|      reasons.push('Current panel scores are incomplete');
+991|    }
+992|    const cap = capacity || vacancyCapacityCounts(vacancy.code);
+993|    if (cap.available <= 0) reasons.push('Vacancy is full');
+994|    return { ok: reasons.length === 0, reasons };
+995|  }
+996|
+997|  function isPanelValid(activePanel) {
+998|    const members = new Set(activePanel);
+999|    if (members.size < 2) return false;
+1000|    if (members.size === 1 && members.has('hiring@pellmoor.test')) return 
false;
+1001|    if (members.size >= 2 && members.has('hiring@pellmoor.test') && 
members.size === 1) return false;
+1002|    if (members.size >= 2 && [...members].every((member) => member === 
'hiring@pellmoor.test')) return false;
+1003|    if (members.size >= 2 && members.size === 1) return false;
+1004|    if (members.size >= 2 && [...members].filter((member) => member !== 
'hiring@pellmoor.test').length === 0) return false;
+1005|    return true;
+1006|  }

+1011|  }

+1148|function seedIfNeeded(db) {
+1149|  const existing = db.prepare('SELECT value FROM metadata WHERE key = 
?').get('seed_complete');
+1150|  if (existing) return;
+1151|  const seed = parseJsonFile(SEED_PATH);
+1152|  const seededAt = seed.clock || nowIso();
+1153|  const passwordHash = sha256(seed.seed_password);
+1154|  const tx = db.transaction(() => {
+1155|    for (const person of seed.people) {
+1156|      db.prepare(
+1157|        'INSERT INTO users(email, name, role, password_hash) VALUES(?, ?, 
?, ?)'
+1158|      ).run(person.email, person.name, person.role, passwordHash);
+1159|    }
+1160|    for (const role of seed.roles) {
+1161|      db.prepare(
+1162|        'INSERT INTO vacancies(code, title, team, openings, revision, 
created_at) VALUES(?, ?, ?, ?, 1, ?)'
+1163|      ).run(role.code, role.title, role.team, role.openings, seededAt);
+1164|    }
+1165|    const seq = db.prepare('INSERT INTO counters(name, value) VALUES(?, 
?)').run('candidate_seq', 109);
+1166|    for (const candidate of seed.candidates) {
+1167|      const currentVersion = 1;
+1168|      const createdAt = timeOffset(seededAt, candidate.days_since_applied,
0);
+1169|      const appliedAt = createdAt;
+1170|      db.prepare(
+1171|        `INSERT INTO candidates(id, vacancy_code, name, stage, 
assessment_version, applied_at, created_at, updated_at, created_by)
+1172|         VALUES(?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`
+1173|      ).run(candidate.id, candidate.role, candidate.name, candidate.stage,
currentVersion, appliedAt, createdAt, createdAt, 'seed');
+1174|      const history = candidate.history || [candidate.stage];
+1175|      history.forEach((stage, index) => {
+1176|        insertSeedStageHistory(db, candidate.id, candidate.role, stage, 
currentVersion, timeOffset(appliedAt, 0, index * 10));
+1177|        insertSeedActivity(db, candidate.role, candidate.id, 'seed', 
'seed-stage', `${candidate.name}: ${stage}`, `Imported stage ${stage}`, 1, {
+1178|          stage,
+1179|          candidateId: candidate.id,
+1180|          assessmentVersion: currentVersion,
+1181|          seed: true,
+1182|        }, timeOffset(appliedAt, 0, index * 10));
+1183|      });
+1184|    }
+1185|    for (const panel of seed.panels) {
+1186|      panel.members.forEach((memberEmail) => {
+1187|        db.prepare(
+1188|          `INSERT INTO candidate_panels(candidate_id, vacancy_code, 
member_email, active, added_by, added_at, removed_by, removed_at)
+1189|           VALUES(?, ?, ?, 1, ?, ?, NULL, NULL)`
+1190|        ).run(panel.candidate, candidateRole(db, panel.candidate), 
memberEmail, 'seed', seededAt);
+1191|        insertSeedActivity(db, candidateRole(db, panel.candidate), 
panel.candidate, 'seed', 'seed-panel', `${panel.candidate}: panel 
${memberEmail}`, `Imported panel member ${memberEmail}`, 1, {
+1192|          candidateId: panel.candidate,
+1193|          memberEmail,
+1194|          seed: true,
+1195|        }, seededAt);
+1196|      });
+1197|    }
+1198|    for (const score of seed.scores) {
+1199|      db.prepare(
+1200|        `INSERT INTO candidate_scores(candidate_id, vacancy_code, 
assessment_version, member_email, score, recorded_by, recorded_at)
+1201|         VALUES(?, ?, 1, ?, ?, ?, ?)`
+1202|      ).run(score.candidate, candidateRole(db, score.candidate), 
score.panel_member, score.score, 'seed', seededAt);
+1203|      insertSeedActivity(db, candidateRole(db, score.candidate), 
score.candidate, 'seed', 'seed-score', `${score.candidate}: score 
${score.score}`, `Imported score ${score.score} from ${score.panel_member}`, 1, 
{
+1204|        candidateId: score.candidate,
+1205|        memberEmail: score.panel_member,
+1206|        score: score.score,
+1207|        assessmentVersion: 1,
+1208|        seed: true,

?)').run('seed_complete', '1');
+1212|  });
+1213|  tx();

+1230|function timeOffset(baseIso, days, minutes) {
+1231|  const base = new Date(baseIso);
+1232|  base.setUTCDate(base.getUTCDate() - Number(days || 0));
+1233|  base.setUTCMinutes(base.getUTCMinutes() + Number(minutes || 0));
+1234|  return base.toISOString();

  function createCandidate(actor, vacancyCode, name, noteText, operationId) {
    return runWriteOperation(actor, 'POST', 
`/api/vacancies/${vacancyCode}/candidates`, operationId, { vacancyCode, name, 
noteText }, () => {
      const vacancy = getVacancyRecord(vacancyCode);
      if (!vacancy) return businessReject(404, 'Vacancy not found');
      const cleanName = normalizeName(name);
      if (!cleanName) return businessReject(400, 'Candidate name is required');
      const duplicate = db
        .prepare('SELECT id FROM candidates WHERE vacancy_code = ? AND 
lower(name) = lower(?)')
        .get(vacancyCode, cleanName);
      if (duplicate) return businessReject(409, 'That person already exists on 
this vacancy');
      const candidateId = nextCandidateId();
      const createdAt = nowIso();
      const appliedAt = createdAt;
      const currentVersion = 1;
      const insertCandidate = db.prepare(
        `INSERT INTO candidates(id, vacancy_code, name, stage, 
assessment_version, applied_at, created_at, updated_at, created_by)
         VALUES(?, ?, ?, 'applied', ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`
      );
      insertCandidate.run(candidateId, vacancyCode, cleanName, currentVersion, 
appliedAt, createdAt, createdAt, actor.email);
      insertStageHistory({
        candidateId,
        vacancyCode,
        stage: 'applied',
        actorEmail: actor.email,
        reason: 'candidate created',
        assessmentVersion: currentVersion,
      });
      insertActivity({
        vacancyCode,
        candidateId,
        actorEmail: actor.email,
        type: 'candidate-created',
        title: `Added ${cleanName}`,
        body: `Created at applied stage`,
        revision: vacancy.revision + 1,
        details: {
          candidateId,
          stage: 'applied',
          assessmentVersion: currentVersion,
        },
      });
      if (noteText && String(noteText).trim()) {
        insertNote({
          actorEmail: actor.email,
          vacancyCode,
          candidateId,
          text: String(noteText).trim(),
        });
      }
      bumpVacancyRevision(vacancyCode);
      return success(201, { vacancy: getVacancyView(vacancyCode), candidate: 
getCandidateView(candidateId) });
    });
  }

  function changeStage(actor, candidateId, nextStage, operationId) {
    return runWriteOperation(actor, 'POST', 
`/api/candidates/${candidateId}/stage`, operationId, { candidateId, nextStage },
() => {
      const candidate = getCandidateForUpdate(candidateId);
      if (!candidate) return businessReject(404, 'Candidate not found');
      const vacancy = getVacancyRecord(candidate.vacancy_code);
      if (!vacancy) return businessReject(404, 'Vacancy not found');
      if (actor.role !== 'hiring manager') return businessReject(403, 'Only Ruth
may move candidates');
      if (candidate.stage === nextStage) return businessReject(409, 'Candidate 
is already at that stage');
      if (TERMINAL_STAGES.includes(candidate.stage)) return businessReject(409, 
'Terminal applications cannot be moved');
      if (![...PIPELINE_STAGES, ...TERMINAL_STAGES].includes(nextStage)) return 
businessReject(400, 'Unknown stage');

      const currentIndex = PIPELINE_STAGES.indexOf(candidate.stage);
      const nextIndex = PIPELINE_STAGES.indexOf(nextStage);
      const terminalMove = TERMINAL_STAGES.includes(nextStage);
      const legalStep = terminalMove || nextIndex === currentIndex - 1 || 
nextIndex === currentIndex + 1;
      if (!legalStep) return businessReject(409, 'Stage jumps are not allowed');

      const releasesOpening = candidate.stage === 'offer' || candidate.stage ===
'hired';
      const takesOpening = nextStage === 'offer';
      const movesIntoInterview = nextStage === 'interview' && candidate.stage 
!== 'interview';
      const beforeVersion = candidate.assessment_version;
      const afterVersion = movesIntoInterview ? beforeVersion + 1 : 
beforeVersion;
      const currentRevision = vacancy.revision;

      if (candidate.stage === 'hired' && nextStage === 'interview') {
        return businessReject(409, 'Use hired to offer first, then offer to 
interview');
      }

      if (takesOpening) {
        const capacity = vacancyCapacityCounts(vacancy.code);
        if (capacity.available <= 0 && candidate.stage !== 'hired') {
          return businessReject(409, 'Vacancy is full');
        }
      }

      if (releasesOpening) {
        releaseOpening(candidate.stage, nextStage);
      }

      db.prepare('UPDATE candidates SET stage = ?, assessment_version = ?, 
updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?').run(nextStage, afterVersion, nowIso(), 
candidateId);
      insertStageHistory({
        candidateId,
        vacancyCode: vacancy.code,
        stage: nextStage,
        actorEmail: actor.email,
        reason: stageReason(candidate.stage, nextStage),
        assessmentVersion: afterVersion,
      });
      insertActivity({
        vacancyCode: vacancy.code,
        candidateId,
        actorEmail: actor.email,
        type: 'stage-change',
        title: `${candidate.name}: ${candidate.stage} → ${nextStage}`,
        body: movesIntoInterview ? `Assessment version advanced to 
${afterVersion}` : `Stage updated without changing assessment version`,
        revision: currentRevision + 1,
        details: {
          fromStage: candidate.stage,
          toStage: nextStage,
          assessmentVersionBefore: beforeVersion,
          assessmentVersionAfter: afterVersion,
          assessmentVersionChanged: movesIntoInterview,
        },
      });
      bumpVacancyRevision(vacancy.code);
      return success(200, { vacancy: getVacancyView(vacancy.code), candidate: 
getCandidateView(candidateId) });
    });
  }

  function changePanel(actor, candidateId, memberEmail, action, operationId) {
    return runWriteOperation(actor, 'POST', 
`/api/candidates/${candidateId}/panel`, operationId, { candidateId, memberEmail,
action }, () => {
      const candidate = getCandidateForUpdate(candidateId);
      if (!candidate) return businessReject(404, 'Candidate not found');
      const vacancy = getVacancyRecord(candidate.vacancy_code);
      if (!vacancy) return businessReject(404, 'Vacancy not found');
      if (actor.role !== 'coordinator') return businessReject(403, 'Only Cal may
change panels');
      if (!['applied', 'screening', 'interview'].includes(candidate.stage)) {
        return businessReject(409, 'Panels are frozen at offer, hired, rejected 
and withdrawn');
      }
      if (!ELIGIBLE_PANEL_MEMBERS.has(memberEmail)) return businessReject(409, 
'That person cannot be assigned to a panel');
      if (memberEmail === 'coord@pellmoor.test') return businessReject(409, 'Cal
cannot be assigned to a panel');
      const currentActive = getActivePanelMembers(candidateId);
      const alreadyActive = currentActive.includes(memberEmail);
      if (action === 'add' && alreadyActive) return businessReject(409, 'That 
panel member is already assigned');
      if (action === 'remove' && !alreadyActive) return businessReject(409, 
'That panel member is not assigned');
      const beforeVersion = candidate.assessment_version;
      const afterVersion = beforeVersion + 1;
      if (action === 'add') {
        db.prepare(
          `INSERT INTO candidate_panels(candidate_id, vacancy_code, 
member_email, active, added_by, added_at, removed_by, removed_at)
           VALUES(?, ?, ?, 1, ?, ?, NULL, NULL)
           ON CONFLICT(candidate_id, member_email) DO UPDATE SET active = 1, 
added_by = excluded.added_by, added_at = excluded.added_at, removed_by = NULL, 
removed_at = NULL`
        ).run(candidateId, vacancy.code, memberEmail, actor.email, nowIso());
      } else {
        db.prepare(
          `UPDATE candidate_panels SET active = 0, removed_by = ?, removed_at = 
? WHERE candidate_id = ? AND member_email = ?`
        ).run(actor.email, nowIso(), candidateId, memberEmail);
      }
      db.prepare('UPDATE candidates SET assessment_version = ?, updated_at = ? 
WHERE id = ?').run(afterVersion, nowIso(), candidateId);
      insertStageHistory({
        candidateId,
        vacancyCode: vacancy.code,
        stage: candidate.stage,
        actorEmail: actor.email,
        reason: action === 'add' ? `panel member ${memberEmail} added` : `panel 
member ${memberEmail} removed`,
        assessmentVersion: afterVersion,
      });
      insertActivity({
        vacancyCode: vacancy.code,
        candidateId,
        actorEmail: actor.email,
        type: action === 'add' ? 'panel-add' : 'panel-remove',
        title: `${action === 'add' ? 'Added' : 'Removed'} panel member`,
        body: `${memberLabel(memberEmail)} ${action === 'add' ? 'assigned to' : 
'removed from'} the panel`,
        revision: vacancy.revision + 1,
        details: {
          memberEmail,
          action,
          assessmentVersionBefore: beforeVersion,
          assessmentVersionAfter: afterVersion,
        },
      });
      bumpVacancyRevision(vacancy.code);
      return success(200, { vacancy: getVacancyView(vacancy.code), candidate: 
getCandidateView(candidateId) });
    });
  }

  function recordScore(actor, candidateId, score, operationId) {
    return runWriteOperation(actor, 'POST', 
`/api/candidates/${candidateId}/scores`, operationId, { candidateId, score }, ()
=> {
      const candidate = getCandidateForUpdate(candidateId);
      if (!candidate) return businessReject(404, 'Candidate not found');
      const vacancy = getVacancyRecord(candidate.vacancy_code);
      if (!vacancy) return businessReject(404, 'Vacancy not found');
      if (candidate.stage !== 'interview') return businessReject(409, 'Scores 
can only be recorded at interview');
      if (!ELIGIBLE_PANEL_MEMBERS.has(actor.email)) return businessReject(403, 
'Only panel members may score');
      const activePanel = getActivePanelMembers(candidateId);
      if (!activePanel.includes(actor.email)) return businessReject(403, 'Only 
assigned panel members may score');
      if (!Number.isInteger(score) || score < 1 || score > 5) return 
businessReject(400, 'Scores must be whole numbers from 1 to 5');
      const version = candidate.assessment_version;
      const existing = db
        .prepare('SELECT score FROM candidate_scores WHERE candidate_id = ? AND 
assessment_version = ? AND member_email = ?')
        .get(candidateId, version, actor.email);
      db.prepare(
        `INSERT INTO candidate_scores(candidate_id, vacancy_code, 
assessment_version, member_email, score, recorded_by, recorded_at)
         VALUES(?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
         ON CONFLICT(candidate_id, assessment_version, member_email)
         DO UPDATE SET score = excluded.score, recorded_by = 
excluded.recorded_by, recorded_at = excluded.recorded_at`
      ).run(candidateId, vacancy.code, version, actor.email, score, actor.email,
nowIso());
      insertActivity({
        vacancyCode: vacancy.code,
        candidateId,
        actorEmail: actor.email,
        type: existing ? 'score-updated' : 'score-recorded',
        title: `${memberLabel(actor.email)} scored ${score}`,
        body: `Assessment version ${version} now records a ${score} from 
${memberLabel(actor.email)}`,
        revision: vacancy.revision + 1,
        details: {
          memberEmail: actor.email,
          score,
          assessmentVersion: version,
          updated: Boolean(existing),
        },
      });
      bumpVacancyRevision(vacancy.code);
      return success(200, { vacancy: getVacancyView(vacancy.code), candidate: 
getCandidateView(candidateId) });
    });
  }

  function addNote(actor, candidateId, text, operationId) {
    return runWriteOperation(actor, 'POST', 
`/api/candidates/${candidateId}/notes`, operationId, { candidateId, text }, () 
=> {
      const candidate = getCandidateForUpdate(candidateId);
      if (!candidate) return businessReject(404, 'Candidate not found');
      const vacancy = getVacancyRecord(candidate.vacancy_code);
      if (!vacancy) return businessReject(404, 'Vacancy not found');
      const cleanText = normalizeNote(text);
      if (!cleanText) return businessReject(400, 'Note text is required');
      const noteId = insertNote({
        actorEmail: actor.email,
        vacancyCode: vacancy.code,
        candidateId,
        text: cleanText,
      });
      insertActivity({
        vacancyCode: vacancy.code,
        candidateId,
        actorEmail: actor.email,
        type: 'note',
        title: `Note added`,
        body: cleanText,
        revision: vacancy.revision + 1,
        details: { noteId },
      });
      bumpVacancyRevision(vacancy.code);
      return success(201, { vacancy: getVacancyView(vacancy.code), candidate: 
getCandidateView(candidateId) });
    });
  }

  function batchPreview(actor, vacancyCode, candidateIds) {
    if (actor.role !== 'hiring manager') {
      return businessReject(403, 'Only Ruth may review batch offers');
    }
    const vacancy = getVacancyRecord(vacancyCode);
    if (!vacancy) return businessReject(404, 'Vacancy not found');
    const selection = candidateIds.map((id) => getCandidateView(id));
    if (selection.some((item) => !item)) return businessReject(404, 'One or more
candidates were not found');
    const currentCapacity = vacancyCapacityCounts(vacancyCode);
    const projectedCapacity = {
      reserved: currentCapacity.reserved + candidateIds.length,
      filled: currentCapacity.filled,
      available: vacancy.openings - currentCapacity.reserved - 
currentCapacity.filled - candidateIds.length,
    };
    return success(200, {
      vacancy: summarizeVacancy(vacancyCode, vacancy, true),
      selectionCount: candidateIds.length,
      selection: selection.map((candidate) => {
        const eligibility = evaluateOfferEligibility(candidate, vacancy);
        return {
          id: candidate.id,
          name: candidate.name,
          assessmentVersion: candidate.assessmentVersion,
          stage: candidate.stage,
          eligible: eligibility.ok,
          reasons: eligibility.reasons,
        };
      }),
      currentCapacity,
      projectedCapacity,
    }, false);
  }

  function confirmBatch(actor, vacancyCode, candidateIds, operationId) {
    return runWriteOperation(actor, 'POST', 
`/api/vacancies/${vacancyCode}/batch-offers`, operationId, { vacancyCode, 
candidateIds }, () => {
      if (actor.role !== 'hiring manager') return businessReject(403, 'Only Ruth
may confirm batch offers');
      const vacancy = getVacancyRecord(vacancyCode);
      if (!vacancy) return businessReject(404, 'Vacancy not found');
      if (!Array.isArray(candidateIds) || candidateIds.length === 0) return 
businessReject(400, 'Select at least one candidate');
      if (candidateIds.some((id) => typeof id !== 'string' || !id.trim())) 
return businessReject(400, 'Candidate identifiers must be non-empty strings');
      if (new Set(candidateIds).size !== candidateIds.length) return 
businessReject(409, 'Duplicate candidate identifiers are not allowed');
      const currentCapacity = vacancyCapacityCounts(vacancyCode);
      if (candidateIds.length > currentCapacity.available) return 
businessReject(409, 'Vacancy does not have enough available openings');

      const selected = candidateIds.map((id) => getCandidateForUpdate(id));
      if (selected.some((candidate) => !candidate)) return businessReject(404, 
'One or more candidates were not found');
      if (selected.some((candidate) => candidate.vacancy_code !== vacancyCode)) 
return businessReject(409, 'All candidates must belong to the selected 
vacancy');
      const stagingProblem = selected.find((candidate) => candidate.stage !== 
'interview');
      if (stagingProblem) return businessReject(409, `${stagingProblem.name} is 
not at interview`);
      for (const candidate of selected) {
        const eligibility = evaluateOfferEligibility(candidate, vacancy);
        if (!eligibility.ok) return businessReject(409, `${candidate.name} is 
not eligible for offer: ${eligibility.reasons.join('; ')}`);
      }
      const batchId = crypto.randomUUID();
      const currentRevision = vacancy.revision;
      const now = nowIso();
      selected.forEach((candidate, index) => {
        db.prepare('UPDATE candidates SET stage = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = 
?').run('offer', now, candidate.id);
        insertStageHistory({
          candidateId: candidate.id,
          vacancyCode,
          stage: 'offer',
          actorEmail: actor.email,
          reason: 'batch offer confirmed',
          assessmentVersion: candidate.assessmentVersion,
          batchId,
          batchPosition: index + 1,
          batchSize: selected.length,
        });
        insertActivity({
          vacancyCode,
          candidateId: candidate.id,
          actorEmail: actor.email,
          type: 'batch-offer',
          title: `${candidate.name} offered in batch ${batchId}`,
          body: `Batch offer ${index + 1} of ${selected.length}`,
          revision: currentRevision + 1,
          batchId,
          batchPosition: index + 1,
          batchSize: selected.length,
          details: {
            candidateId: candidate.id,
            assessmentVersion: candidate.assessmentVersion,
            batchId,
            batchPosition: index + 1,
            batchSize: selected.length,
            toStage: 'offer',
          },
        });
      });
      bumpVacancyRevision(vacancyCode);
      return success(200, {
        vacancy: getVacancyView(vacancyCode),
        batchId,
        selection: selected.map((candidate, index) => ({
          id: candidate.id,
          name: candidate.name,
          position: index + 1,
          total: selected.length,
        })),
      });
    });
  }

  function buildCandidateDetail(candidateRow) {
    const activePanel = getActivePanelMembers(candidateRow.id);
    const candidatePanel = getPanelRows(candidateRow.id);
    const currentScores = getCurrentScores(candidateRow.id, 
candidateRow.assessment_version);
    const historicalScores = getHistoricalScores(candidateRow.id, 
candidateRow.assessment_version);
    const notes = getNotes(candidateRow.id);
    const activity = getActivity(candidateRow.vacancy_code, candidateRow.id);
    const vacancy = getVacancyRecord(candidateRow.vacancy_code);
    const capacity = vacancyCapacityCounts(candidateRow.vacancy_code);
    const eligibility = evaluateOfferEligibility({
      id: candidateRow.id,
      name: candidateRow.name,
      stage: candidateRow.stage,
      assessmentVersion: candidateRow.assessment_version,
      activePanel,
      currentScores,
    }, vacancy, capacity);
    return {
      id: candidateRow.id,
      name: candidateRow.name,
      stage: candidateRow.stage,
      assessmentVersion: candidateRow.assessment_version,
      vacancy: {
        code: candidateRow.vacancy_code,
        title: candidateRow.vacancy_title,
        team: candidateRow.vacancy_team,
        openings: candidateRow.openings,
        revision: candidateRow.revision,
      },
      appliedAt: candidateRow.applied_at,
      createdAt: candidateRow.created_at,
      updatedAt: candidateRow.updated_at,
      stageHistory: db
        .prepare(
          `SELECT stage, actor_email, reason, assessment_version, batch_id, 
batch_position, batch_size, created_at
           FROM candidate_stage_history
           WHERE candidate_id = ?
           ORDER BY id ASC`
        )
        .all(candidateRow.id)

          stage: row.stage,
          actorEmail: row.actor_email,
          reason: row.reason,
          assessmentVersion: row.assessment_version,
          batchId: row.batch_id,
          batchPosition: row.batch_position,
          batchSize: row.batch_size,
          createdAt: row.created_at,
        })),
      activePanel,
      panelAssignments: candidatePanel.map((row) => ({
        memberEmail: row.member_email,
        active: Boolean(row.active),
        addedBy: row.added_by,
        addedAt: row.added_at,
        removedBy: row.removed_by,
        removedAt: row.removed_at,

        blockedReasons: eligibility.reasons,
      },
      canScore: activePanel.includes(candidateRow.vacancy_code === 'ROLE-014' ? 
'hiring@pellmoor.test' : ''),
      capacity,
    };
  }

  function groupHistoricalScores(rows) {
    const grouped = new Map();
    for (const row of rows) {
      if (!grouped.has(row.assessment_version)) 
grouped.set(row.assessment_version, []);
      grouped.get(row.assessment_version).push({
        memberEmail: row.member_email,
        score: row.score,
        recordedBy: row.recorded_by,
        recordedAt: row.recorded_at,
      });
    }
    return [...grouped.entries()].map(([assessmentVersion, scores]) => ({
      assessmentVersion,
      scores,
    }));
  }

  function success(statusCode, body, storeReceipt = true) {
    return { statusCode, body, storeReceipt };
  }

  function storedResult(statusCode, responseJson) {
    return { statusCode: Number(statusCode), body: JSON.parse(responseJson), 
storeReceipt: false, replay: true };
  }

  function businessReject(statusCode, message, storeReceipt = true) {
    return success(statusCode, { error: message }, storeReceipt);
  }

  function normalizeName(name) {
    if (typeof name !== 'string') return '';
    return name.trim().replace(/\s+/g, ' ');
  }

  function normalizeNote(text) {
    if (typeof text !== 'string') return '';
    return text.trim();
  }

  function normalizeOperationId(operationId) {
    if (typeof operationId !== 'string') return '';
    const trimmed = operationId.trim();
    return trimmed.length ? trimmed : '';
  }

  function nextCandidateId() {
    const tx = db.transaction(() => {
      const current = db.prepare('SELECT value FROM counters WHERE name = 
?').get('candidate_seq');
      const next = current ? Number(current.value) + 1 : 110;
      db.prepare(
        `INSERT INTO counters(name, value) VALUES('candidate_seq', ?) ON 
CONFLICT(name) DO UPDATE SET value = excluded.value`
      ).run(next);
      return `CAND-${next}`;
    });
    return tx();
  }

  function getCandidateForUpdate(candidateId) {
    return db
      .prepare(
        `SELECT c.id, c.vacancy_code, c.name, c.stage, c.assessment_version, 
c.applied_at, c.created_at, c.updated_at,
                v.openings, v.revision
         FROM candidates c
         JOIN vacancies v ON v.code = c.vacancy_code
         WHERE c.id = ?`
      )
      .get(candidateId);
  }

  function insertStageHistory({ candidateId, vacancyCode, stage, actorEmail, 
reason, assessmentVersion, batchId = null, batchPosition = null, batchSize = 
null }) {
    db.prepare(
      `INSERT INTO candidate_stage_history(candidate_id, vacancy_code, stage, 
actor_email, reason, assessment_version, batch_id, batch_position, batch_size, 
created_at)
       VALUES(?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`
    ).run(candidateId, vacancyCode, stage, actorEmail, reason, 
assessmentVersion, batchId, batchPosition, batchSize, nowIso());
  }

  function insertNote({ actorEmail, vacancyCode, candidateId, text }) {
    const result = db.prepare(
      `INSERT INTO candidate_notes(candidate_id, vacancy_code, actor_email, 
text, created_at)
       VALUES(?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`
    ).run(candidateId, vacancyCode, actorEmail, text, nowIso());
    return result.lastInsertRowid;
  }

  function insertActivity({ vacancyCode, candidateId = null, actorEmail, type, 
title, body, revision, batchId = null, batchPosition = null, batchSize = null, 
details = {} }) {
    db.prepare(
      `INSERT INTO activity_events(vacancy_code, candidate_id, actor_email, 
type, title, body, revision, batch_id, batch_position, batch_size, created_at, 
details_json)
       VALUES(?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`
    ).run(vacancyCode, candidateId, actorEmail, type, title, body, revision, 
batchId, batchPosition, batchSize, nowIso(), JSON.stringify(details));
  }

  function bumpVacancyRevision(vacancyCode) {
    db.prepare('UPDATE vacancies SET revision = revision + 1 WHERE code = 
?').run(vacancyCode);
  }

  function releaseOpening(currentStage, nextStage) {
    if (currentStage === 'offer' || currentStage === 'hired') {
      if (nextStage !== 'offer' && nextStage !== 'hired') {
        return true;
      }
    }
    return false;
  }

  function stageReason(fromStage, toStage) {
    if (TERMINAL_STAGES.includes(toStage)) return `moved to ${toStage}`;
    if (toStage === 'interview' && fromStage !== 'interview') return 'entered 
interview';
    if (fromStage === 'offer' && toStage === 'hired') return 'accepted offer';
    if (fromStage === 'hired' && toStage === 'offer') return 'returned from 
hired to offer';
    return `moved from ${fromStage} to ${toStage}`;
  }

  function getActivePanelMembers(candidateId) {
    return db
      .prepare('SELECT member_email FROM candidate_panels WHERE candidate_id = ?
AND active = 1 ORDER BY member_email')
      .all(candidateId)
      .map((row) => row.member_email);
  }

  function getPanelRows(candidateId) {
    return db
      .prepare(
        `SELECT member_email, active, added_by, added_at, removed_by, removed_at
         FROM candidate_panels
         WHERE candidate_id = ?
         ORDER BY member_email`
      )
      .all(candidateId);
  }

  function getCurrentScores(candidateId, version) {
    return db
      .prepare(
        `SELECT member_email, score, recorded_by, recorded_at
         FROM candidate_scores
         WHERE candidate_id = ? AND assessment_version = ?
         ORDER BY member_email`
      )
      .all(candidateId, version);
  }

  function getHistoricalScores(candidateId, currentVersion) {
    return db
      .prepare(
        `SELECT assessment_version, member_email, score, recorded_by, 
recorded_at
         FROM candidate_scores
         WHERE candidate_id = ? AND assessment_version < ?
         ORDER BY assessment_version DESC, member_email`
      )
      .all(candidateId, currentVersion);
  }

  function getNotes(candidateId) {
    return db
      .prepare(
        `SELECT id, actor_email, text, created_at
         FROM candidate_notes
         WHERE candidate_id = ?
         ORDER BY id ASC`
      )
      .all(candidateId);
  }

  function getActivity(vacancyCode, candidateId = null) {
    if (candidateId) {
      return db
        .prepare(
          `SELECT id, vacancy_code, candidate_id, actor_email, type, title, 
body, revision, batch_id, batch_position, batch_size, created_at, details_json
           FROM activity_events
           WHERE vacancy_code = ? AND candidate_id = ?
           ORDER BY id ASC`
        )
        .all(vacancyCode, candidateId)
        .map(formatActivity);
    }
    return db
      .prepare(
        `SELECT id, vacancy_code, candidate_id, actor_email, type, title, body, 
revision, batch_id, batch_position, batch_size, created_at, details_json
         FROM activity_events
         WHERE vacancy_code = ?
         ORDER BY id DESC
         LIMIT 50`
      )
      .all(vacancyCode)
      .map(formatActivity)
      .reverse();
  }

      vacancyCode: row.vacancy_code,
      candidateId: row.candidate_id,
      actorEmail: row.actor_email,
      type: row.type,

      batchId: row.batch_id,

      if (!Number.isInteger(expectedRevision) || expectedRevision < 0) return 
businessReject(400, 'Expected revision is required');
      if (vacancy.revision !== expectedRevision) {
        return staleReject(vacancyCode, 'Stale revision for candidate 
creation');
      }
      const cleanName = normalizeName(name);
      if (!cleanName) return businessReject(400, 'Candidate name is required');
      const duplicate = db
        .prepare('SELECT id FROM candidates WHERE vacancy_code = ? AND 
lower(name) = lower(?)')
        .get(vacancyCode, cleanName);
      if (duplicate) return businessReject(409, 'That person already exists on 
this vacancy');
      const candidateId = nextCandidateId();
      const createdAt = nowIso();
      const appliedAt = createdAt;
      const currentVersion = 1;
      db.prepare(
        \\`INSERT INTO candidates(id, vacancy_code, name, stage, 
assessment_version, applied_at, created_at, updated_at, created_by)
         VALUES(?, ?, ?, 'applied', ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\\`
      ).run(candidateId, vacancyCode, cleanName, currentVersion, appliedAt, 
createdAt, createdAt, actor.email);
      insertStageHistory({
        candidateId,
        vacancyCode,
        stage: 'applied',
        actorEmail: actor.email,
        reason: 'candidate created',
        assessmentVersion: currentVersion,
      });
      insertActivity({
        vacancyCode,
        candidateId,
        actorEmail: actor.email,
        type: 'candidate-created',
        title: `Added ${cleanName}`,
        body: `Created at applied stage`,
        revision: vacancy.revision + 1,
        details: {
          candidateId,
          stage: 'applied',
          assessmentVersion: currentVersion,
        },
      });
      if (noteText && String(noteText).trim()) {
        const noteId = insertNote({
          actorEmail: actor.email,
          vacancyCode,
          candidateId,
          text: String(noteText).trim(),
        });
        insertActivity({
          vacancyCode,
          candidateId,
          actorEmail: actor.email,
          type: 'note',
          title: 'Initial note added',
          body: String(noteText).trim(),
          revision: vacancy.revision + 1,
          details: { noteId },
        });
      }
      bumpVacancyRevision(vacancyCode);
      return success(201, { vacancy: getVacancyView(vacancyCode), candidate: 
getCandidateView(candidateId) });
    });
  }

  function changeStage`
);

s = s.replace(
  /function changeStage\(actor, candidateId, nextStage, operationId\) 
\{[\s\S]*?\n  \}\n\n  function changePanel/,
`function changeStage(actor, candidateId, nextStage, expectedRevision, 
operationId) {
    return runWriteOperation(actor, 'POST', 
\`/api/candidates/${candidateId}/stage\`, operationId, { candidateId, nextStage,
expectedRevision }, () => {
      const candidate = getCandidateForUpdate(candidateId);
      if (!candidate) return businessReject(404, 'Candidate not found');
      const vacancy = getVacancyRecord(candidate.vacancy_code);
      if (!vacancy) return businessReject(404, 'Vacancy not found');
      if (!Number.isInteger(expectedRevision) || expectedRevision < 0) return 
businessReject(400, 'Expected revision is required');
      if (vacancy.revision !== expectedRevision) return 
staleReject(vacancy.code, 'Stale revision for stage change');
      if (actor.role !== 'hiring manager') return businessReject(403, 'Only Ruth
may move candidates');
      if (candidate.stage === nextStage) return businessReject(409, 'Candidate 
is already at that stage');
      if (TERMINAL_STAGES.includes(candidate.stage)) return businessReject(409, 
'Terminal applications cannot be moved');
      if (![...PIPELINE_STAGES, ...TERMINAL_STAGES].includes(nextStage)) return 
businessReject(400, 'Unknown stage');

      const currentIndex = PIPELINE_STAGES.indexOf(candidate.stage);
      const nextIndex = PIPELINE_STAGES.indexOf(nextStage);
      const terminalMove = TERMINAL_STAGES.includes(nextStage);
      const legalStep = terminalMove || nextIndex === currentIndex - 1 || 
nextIndex === currentIndex + 1;
      if (!legalStep) return businessReject(409, 'Stage jumps are not allowed');

      const releasesOpening = candidate.stage === 'offer' || candidate.stage ===
'hired';
      const takesOpening = nextStage === 'offer';
      const movesIntoInterview = nextStage === 'interview' && candidate.stage 
!== 'interview';
      const beforeVersion = candidate.assessment_version;
      const afterVersion = movesIntoInterview ? beforeVersion + 1 : 
beforeVersion;
      const currentRevision = vacancy.revision;

      if (candidate.stage === 'hired' && nextStage === 'interview') {
        return businessReject(409, 'Use hired to offer first, then offer to 
interview');
      }

      if (takesOpening) {
        const capacity = vacancyCapacityCounts(vacancy.code);
        if (capacity.available <= 0 && candidate.stage !== 'hired') {
          return businessReject(409, 'Vacancy is full');
        }
      }

      db.prepare('UPDATE candidates SET stage = ?, assessment_version = ?, 
updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?').run(nextStage, afterVersion, nowIso(), 
candidateId);
      insertStageHistory({
        candidateId,
        vacancyCode: vacancy.code,
        stage: nextStage,
        actorEmail: actor.email,
        reason: stageReason(candidate.stage, nextStage),
        assessmentVersion: afterVersion,
      });
      insertActivity({
        vacancyCode: vacancy.code,
        candidateId,
        actorEmail: actor.email,
        type: 'stage-change',
        title: `${candidate.name}: ${candidate.stage} → ${nextStage}`,
        body: movesIntoInterview ? `Assessment version advanced to 
${afterVersion}` : `Stage updated without changing assessment version`,
        revision: currentRevision + 1,
        details: {
          fromStage: candidate.stage,
          toStage: nextStage,
          assessmentVersionBefore: beforeVersion,
          assessmentVersionAfter: afterVersion,
          assessmentVersionChanged: movesIntoInterview,
          openingReleased: releasesOpening && !takesOpening,
        },
      });
      bumpVacancyRevision(vacancy.code);
      return success(200, { vacancy: getVacancyView(vacancy.code), candidate: 
getCandidateView(candidateId) });
    });
  }

  function changePanel`

      if (action === 'remove' && !alreadyActive) return businessReject(409,

      const beforeVersion = candidate.assessment_version;
      const afterVersion = beforeVersion + 1;
      if (action === 'add') {
        db.prepare(
          \\`INSERT INTO candidate_panels(candidate_id, vacancy_code, 
member_email, active, added_by, added_at, removed_by, removed_at)

        candidateId,
        vacancyCode: vacancy.code,
        stage: candidate.stage,
        actorEmail: actor.email,
        reason: action === 'add' ? `panel member ${memberEmail} added` : `panel 
member ${memberEmail} removed`,
        assessmentVersion: afterVersion,
      });
      insertActivity({
        vacancyCode: vacancy.code,
        candidateId,
        actorEmail: actor.email,
        type: action === 'add' ? 'panel-add' : 'panel-remove',
        title: `${action === 'add' ? 'Added' : 'Removed'} panel member`,
        body: `${memberLabel(memberEmail)} ${action === 'add' ? 'assigned to' : 
'removed from'} the panel`,
        revision: vacancy.revision + 1,
        details: {
          memberEmail,
          action,
          assessmentVersionBefore: beforeVersion,
          assessmentVersionAfter: afterVersion,
        },
      });
      bumpVacancyRevision(vacancy.code);
      return success(200, { vacancy: getVacancyView(vacancy.code), candidate: 
getCandidateView(candidateId) });
    });

s = s.replace(
  /function confirmBatch\(actor, vacancyCode, candidateIds, operationId\) 
\{[\s\S]*?\n  \}\n\n  function runWriteOperation/,
`function confirmBatch(actor, vacancyCode, candidateIds, expectedRevision, 
operationId) {
    return runWriteOperation(actor, 'POST', 
\`/api/vacancies/${vacancyCode}/batch-offers\`, operationId, { vacancyCode, 
candidateIds, expectedRevision }, () => {
      if (actor.role !== 'hiring manager') return businessReject(403, 'Only Ruth
may confirm batch offers');
      const vacancy = getVacancyRecord(vacancyCode);
      if (!vacancy) return businessReject(404, 'Vacancy not found');
      if (!Number.isInteger(expectedRevision) || expectedRevision < 0) return 
businessReject(400, 'Expected revision is required');
      if (vacancy.revision !== expectedRevision) return 
staleReject(vacancy.code, 'Stale revision for batch confirmation');
      if (!Array.isArray(candidateIds) || candidateIds.length === 0) return 
businessReject(400, 'Select at least one candidate');
      if (candidateIds.some((id) => typeof id !== 'string' || !id.trim())) 
return businessReject(400, 'Candidate identifiers must be non-empty strings');
      if (new Set(candidateIds).size !== candidateIds.length) return 
businessReject(409, 'Duplicate candidate identifiers are not allowed');
      const currentCapacity = vacancyCapacityCounts(vacancyCode);
      if (candidateIds.length > currentCapacity.available) return 
businessReject(409, 'Vacancy does not have enough available openings');

      const selected = candidateIds.map((id) => getCandidateForUpdate(id));
      if (selected.some((candidate) => !candidate)) return businessReject(404, 
'One or more candidates were not found');
      if (selected.some((candidate) => candidate.vacancy_code !== vacancyCode)) 
return businessReject(409, 'All candidates must belong to the selected 
vacancy');
      const stagingProblem = selected.find((candidate) => candidate.stage !== 
'interview');
      if (stagingProblem) return businessReject(409, `${stagingProblem.name} is 
not at interview`);
      for (const candidate of selected) {
        const eligibility = evaluateOfferEligibility(candidate, vacancy);
        if (!eligibility.ok) return businessReject(409, `${candidate.name} is 
not eligible for offer: ${eligibility.reasons.join('; ')}`);
      }
      const batchId = crypto.randomUUID();
      const currentRevision = vacancy.revision;
      const now = nowIso();
      selected.forEach((candidate, index) => {
        db.prepare('UPDATE candidates SET stage = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = 
?').run('offer', now, candidate.id);
        insertStageHistory({
          candidateId: candidate.id,
          vacancyCode,
          stage: 'offer',
          actorEmail: actor.email,
          reason: 'batch offer confirmed',
          assessmentVersion: candidate.assessmentVersion,
          batchId,
          batchPosition: index + 1,
          batchSize: selected.length,
        });
        insertActivity({
          vacancyCode,
          candidateId: candidate.id,
          actorEmail: actor.email,
          type: 'batch-offer',
          title: `${candidate.name} offered in batch ${batchId}`,
          body: `Batch offer ${index + 1} of ${selected.length}`,
          revision: currentRevision + 1,
          batchId,
          batchPosition: index + 1,
          batchSize: selected.length,
          details: {
            candidateId: candidate.id,
            assessmentVersion: candidate.assessmentVersion,
            batchId,
            batchPosition: index + 1,
            batchSize: selected.length,
            toStage: 'offer',
          },
        });
      });
      bumpVacancyRevision(vacancyCode);
      return success(200, {
        vacancy: getVacancyView(vacancyCode),
        batchId,
        selection: selected.map((candidate, index) => ({
          id: candidate.id,
          name: candidate.name,
          position: index + 1,
          total: selected.length,
        })),
      });
    });
  }

  function runWriteOperation`
);

s = s.replace(
  /function buildCandidateDetail\(candidateRow\) \{[\s\S]*?\n      canScore: 
activePanel\.includes\(candidateRow\.vacancy_code === 'ROLE-014' \? 
'hiring@pellmoor\.test' : ''\),\n      capacity,\n    \};\n  \}/,
`function buildCandidateDetail(candidateRow) {
    const activePanel = getActivePanelMembers(candidateRow.id);
    const candidatePanel = getPanelRows(candidateRow.id);
    const currentScores = getCurrentScores(candidateRow.id, 
candidateRow.assessment_version);
    const historicalScores = getHistoricalScores(candidateRow.id, 
candidateRow.assessment_version);
    const notes = getNotes(candidateRow.id);
    const activity = getActivity(candidateRow.vacancy_code, candidateRow.id);
    const vacancy = getVacancyRecord(candidateRow.vacancy_code);
    const capacity = vacancyCapacityCounts(candidateRow.vacancy_code);
    const eligibility = evaluateOfferEligibility({
      id: candidateRow.id,
      name: candidateRow.name,
      stage: candidateRow.stage,
      assessmentVersion: candidateRow.assessment_version,
      activePanel,
      currentScores,
    }, vacancy, capacity);
    return {
      id: candidateRow.id,
      name: candidateRow.name,
      stage: candidateRow.stage,
      assessmentVersion: candidateRow.assessment_version,
      vacancy: {
        code: candidateRow.vacancy_code,
        title: candidateRow.vacancy_title,
        team: candidateRow.vacancy_team,
        openings: candidateRow.openings,
        revision: candidateRow.revision,
      },
      appliedAt: candidateRow.applied_at,

      updatedAt: candidateRow.updated_at,
      stageHistory: db
        .prepare(
          \\`SELECT stage, actor_email, reason, assessment_version, batch_id, 
batch_position, batch_size, created_at
           FROM candidate_stage_history
           WHERE candidate_id = ?
           ORDER BY id ASC\\`
        )
        .all(candidateRow.id)
        .map((row) => ({
          stage: row.stage,

          assessmentVersion: row.assessment_version,
          batchId: row.batch_id,
          batchPosition: row.batch_position,

          createdAt: row.created_at,

      activePanel,
      panelAssignments: candidatePanel.map((row) => ({
        memberEmail: row.member_email,
        active: Boolean(row.active),
        addedBy: row.added_by,
        addedAt: row.added_at,
        removedBy: row.removed_by,
        removedAt: row.removed_at,
      })),
      currentScores,
      historicalScores: groupHistoricalScores(historicalScores),
      notes,
      activity,
      offer: {
        ready: eligibility.ok,
        blockedReasons: eligibility.reasons,
      },
      canScore: false,
      capacity,
    };
  }`
);

s = s.replace(
  /function isPanelValid\(activePanel\) \{[\s\S]*?\n  \}/,
`function isPanelValid(activePanel) {
    const members = new Set(activePanel);
    if (members.size < 2) return false;
    if (![...members].some((member) => member !== 'hiring@pellmoor.test')) 
return false;
    return true;
  }`
);

s = s.replace(
  /function evaluateOfferEligibility\(candidate, vacancy, capacity = null\) 
\{[\s\S]*?\n  \}/,
`function evaluateOfferEligibility(candidate, vacancy, capacity = null) {
    const reasons = [];
    if (!candidate || !vacancy) {
      return { ok: false, reasons: ['Missing candidate or vacancy'] };
    }
    if (candidate.stage !== 'interview') reasons.push('Candidate is not at 
interview');
    const activePanel = candidate.activePanel || 
getActivePanelMembers(candidate.id);
    if (!isPanelValid(activePanel)) reasons.push('Panel is not complete');
    const currentScores = candidate.currentScores || 
getCurrentScores(candidate.id, candidate.assessmentVersion);
    const missing = activePanel.filter((memberEmail) => 
!currentScores.find((row) => row.member_email === memberEmail));
    if (missing.length > 0) reasons.push('Current panel scores are incomplete');
    const cap = capacity || vacancyCapacityCounts(vacancy.code);
    if (cap.available <= 0) reasons.push('Vacancy is full');
    return { ok: reasons.length === 0, reasons };
  }`
);

s = s.replace(
  /function runWriteOperation\(actor, method, route, operationId, payload, 
handler\) \{[\s\S]*?\n  \}/,
`function runWriteOperation(actor, method, route, operationId, payload, handler)
{
    const cleanOperationId = normalizeOperationId(operationId);
    if (!cleanOperationId) return businessReject(400, 'Operation id is 
required', false);
    const hash = payloadHash({ method, route, payload });
    const existing = db
      .prepare(
        \\`SELECT status_code, response_json, payload_hash
         FROM operations
         WHERE actor_email = ? AND operation_id = ? AND method = ? AND route = 
?\\`
      )
      .get(actor.email, cleanOperationId, method, route);
    if (existing) {
      if (existing.payload_hash !== hash) {
        return businessReject(409, 'That retry identity was already used for 
different input');
      }
      return storedResult(existing.status_code, existing.response_json);
    }
    const tx = db.transaction(() => {
      const repeat = db
        .prepare(
          \\`SELECT status_code, response_json, payload_hash
           FROM operations
           WHERE actor_email = ? AND operation_id = ? AND method = ? AND route =
?\\`
        )
        .get(actor.email, cleanOperationId, method, route);
      if (repeat) {
        if (repeat.payload_hash !== hash) {
          return storedResult(409, JSON.stringify({ error: 'That retry identity 
was already used for different input' }));
        }
        return storedResult(repeat.status_code, repeat.response_json);
      }
      const result = handler();
      if (result.storeReceipt !== false) {
        db.prepare(
          \\`INSERT INTO operations(actor_email, operation_id, method, route, 
payload_hash, status_code, response_json, created_at)
           VALUES(?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\\`
        ).run(actor.email, cleanOperationId, method, route, hash, 
result.statusCode, JSON.stringify(result.body), nowIso());
      }
      return result;
    });
    return tx();
  }`
);

s = s.replace(
  /function nextCandidateId\(\) \{[\s\S]*?\n  \}/,
`function nextCandidateId() {
    const tx = db.transaction(() => {
      const current = db.prepare('SELECT value FROM counters WHERE name = 
?').get('candidate_seq');
      const next = current ? Number(current.value) + 1 : 110;
      db.prepare(
        \\`INSERT INTO counters(name, value) VALUES('candidate_seq', ?) ON 
CONFLICT(name) DO UPDATE SET value = excluded.value\\`
      ).run(next);
      return \\`CAND-${next}\\`;
    });
    return tx();
  }`
);

s = s.replace(
  /function businessReject\(statusCode, message, storeReceipt = true\) \{\n    
return success\(statusCode, \{ error: message \}, storeReceipt\);\n  \}/,
`function businessReject(statusCode, message, storeReceipt = true, extra = {}) {
    return success(statusCode, { error: message, ...extra }, storeReceipt);
  }
`
);

      candidateId: row.candidate_id,
      actorEmail: row.actor_email,
      type: row.type,
      title: row.title,
      body: row.body,
      revision: row.revision,
      batchId: row.batch_id,
      batchPosition: row.batch_position,
      batchSize: row.batch_size,
      createdAt: row.created_at,
      details: JSON.parse(row.details_json || '{}'),
    };
  }`
);

s = s.replace(
